汉坦病毒本期文献呈现多方向研究进展

本期重点文献按发表日期、相关性、证据等级和研究质量综合排序，研究结果与综述证据分别核验后形成以下进展。

  • 236 名完整案例受访者的知识水平较低(平均 8.13/20,标准差 5.84),其中 59.0% 的人知识水平较低。态度中等(平均 33.34/50,SD 7.78),总体实践较高(平均 42.98/64,SD 15.83)。跨领域的内部一致性很强(alpha 0.886-0.967)。 [paper-429d41dae56ad7f4]
  • 在 2208 条数据库记录中,经过删除 976 条重复项和筛选后,有 20 条报告被纳入儿科核心综合报告中。报告集中在美洲。复发特征包括发烧、胃肠道症状、肌痛、血小板减少、血液浓缩、肺水肿、呼吸衰竭和休克。 [paper-e7f60da441e68eed]
  • 血小板减少症是最一致的异常,通常在汉坦 (HTNV)、多布拉瓦 (DOBV) 和首尔 (SEOV) 病毒感染中比普马拉 (PUUV) 病毒感染更为明显,后者与较轻的疾病相关。研究结果强调,与 PUUV 相比,HTNV、DOBV 和 SEOV 感染的血小板减少和出血更为严重。 [paper-5e71221146a431c6]
  • 所提供的证据中没有明确报告主要结果。 [paper-4d1eef53093534ee]
  • 原始证据未提供足以独立概括的稳定共识,具体观点见文章摘要和下方其他要素。 [paper-c4119fa47e14d3a3]

📘 研究型文献 / Research Articles

Journal: The Lancet. Microbe  |  Online: 2026-07-15  |  First published: 2026-07-15  |  Print: 2026-07-15  |  Report date: 2026-07-15  |  DOI: 10.1016/j.lanmic.2026.101454  |  Citation: 2026, 101454
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究 · E1

智利首次对人源安第斯山脉病毒进行全国性全基因组表征:一项回顾性基因组流行病学研究。

First nationwide full-genome characterisation of human-derived Andes virus in Chile: a retrospective genomic epidemiology study.
Authors: Ramos H, Díaz-Gavidia C, Fuentes-Luppichini E, Díaz M, Pradenas J, Rogers C, Pino K, Henríquez C, Martínez-Valdebenito C, Ferrés M
摘要中文翻译背景:安第斯病毒(ANDV)是已知的唯一可在人与人之间传播的汉坦病毒,并在智利和阿根廷引起汉坦病毒心肺综合征。在智利,ANDV 基因组多样性仍未完全表征。本研究旨在利用 13 年期间(2011-24)收集的人类临床样本来表征 ANDV 的遗传多样性、地理结构和分子特征。方法:我们对智利 ANDV 感染进行了回顾性基因组流行病学研究。对 2011 年 3 月 9 日至 2024 年 6 月 27 日期间收集的确诊 ANDV 患者的临床样本进行了分析和测序。临床和流行病学数据来自诊断实验室和监测项目。生成了 S、M 和 L 片段的共有序列,并使用系统发育推断和变体调用来评估遗传聚类和分歧。结果:我们分析了 58 名感染者的临床样本,并确定了 ANDV 的两种主要基因组变异,它们具有不同的地理分布,由 S、M 和 L 区段的核苷酸和氨基酸取代的区域结构模式定义:ANDV Chi-North(智利中部)和 ANDV-South(智利南部)。没有观察到按临床严重程度进行一致的聚类,并且没有反复出现的非同义替换与严重疾病唯一相关。此前在阿根廷疫情中与人际传播相关的替代现象在智利序列中并未得到一致观察,包括四例人际传播病例。尽管 ANDV 样病毒中描述的一些替换存在于 Chi-North 谱系中,但该谱系在系统发育上仍然不同,并且在地理上仅限于智利中部。解释:据我们所知,这项研究首次提供了智利全国范围内人源性 ANDV 的基因组特征。地理结构变异的识别表明,智利的 ANDV 多样性是由区域多样化而非临床结果驱动的。缺乏与疾病严重程度或人际传播相关的一致氨基酸特征表明,这些表型不太可能仅用病毒遗传变异来解释。这些发现完善了目前对 ANDV 进化的理解,并强调了在流行地区持续进行综合基因组监测的必要性。资助:Agencia Nacional de Investigación y Desarrollo de Chile 和国立卫生研究院。

First nationwide full-genome characterisation of human-derived Andes virus in Chile: a retrospective genomic epidemiology study.

Authors: Ramos H, Díaz-Gavidia C, Fuentes-Luppichini E, Díaz M, Pradenas J, Rogers C, Pino K, Henríquez C, Martínez-Valdebenito C, Ferrés M
Original Abstract
BACKGROUND: Andes virus (ANDV) is the only hantavirus known to transmit between humans and causes hantavirus cardiopulmonary syndrome in Chile and Argentina. In Chile, ANDV genomic diversity remains incompletely characterised. This study aimed to characterise the genetic diversity, geographical structure, and molecular signatures of ANDV using human clinical samples collected over a 13-year period (2011-24). METHODS: We conducted a retrospective genomic epidemiology study of ANDV infections in Chile. Clinical samples from patients with confirmed ANDV, collected between March 9, 2011, and June 27, 2024, were analysed and sequenced. Clinical and epidemiological data were obtained from diagnostic laboratories and surveillance programmes. Consensus sequences for the S, M, and L segments were generated, and genetic clustering and divergence were assessed using phylogenetic inference and variant calling. FINDINGS: We analysed clinical samples from 58 infected individuals and identified two major genomic variants of ANDV with distinct geographical distributions, defined by regionally structured patterns of nucleotide and amino acid substitutions across the S, M, and L segments: ANDV Chi-North (central Chile) and ANDV-South (southern Chile). No consistent clustering by clinical severity was observed, and no recurrent non-synonymous substitutions were uniquely associated with severe disease. Substitutions previously associated with person-to-person transmission in outbreaks in Argentina were not consistently observed in Chilean sequences, including in four person-to-person transmission cases. Although some substitutions described in ANDV-like viruses were present in the Chi-North lineage, this lineage remained phylogenetically distinct and geographically restricted to central Chile. INTERPRETATION: To our knowledge, this study provides the first nationwide genomic characterisation of human-derived ANDV in Chile. The identification of geographically structured variants indicates that ANDV diversity in Chile is driven by regional diversification rather than clinical outcome. The absence of consistent amino acid signatures associated with disease severity or person-to-person transmission suggests that these phenotypes are unlikely to be explained by viral genetic variation alone. These findings refine current understanding of ANDV evolution and highlight the need for continued integrated genomic surveillance in endemic regions. FUNDING: Agencia Nacional de Investigación y Desarrollo de Chile and National Institutes of Health.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究 · E2

气候变化中的生态级联和未来汉坦病毒溢出风险

Ecological Cascades and Future Hantavirus Spillover Risk in a Changing Climate
Authors: Pranav Pandit, Andie H. Jian, Ayushi Gupta, Pranav Kulkarni, Pandit PS, Jian AH, Gupta A, Kulkarni PS, Pranav S. Pandit, Pranav S. Kulkarni
摘要中文翻译摘要 人为气候变化正在重新调整全球生物多样性,并加速人类与野生动物界面的人畜共患病溢出。2026 年多国游轮爆发的安第斯病毒凸显了啮齿动物传播的正汉坦病毒所带来的不可预测的全球性威胁,这种病毒在全世界范围内引起严重、高度致命的人类疾病。尽管存在重大公共卫生风险,但仍缺乏将环境驱动因素与汉坦病毒危害的系统性变化联系起来的宏观生态综合。在这里,我们实施了一个综合的生态和机器学习框架,将 61 个宿主物种的 89 个独特的病毒宿主关联与机械感染力 (FOI) 模型相结合,以预测 2041-2060 年当前和未来气候情景(SSP2-4.5 和 SSP5-8.5)下的全球溢出危害。我们的模型表明,溢出危害在空间上不均匀但在地理上广泛扩展,大约 74.9% 的模型土地面积经历了 FOI 的增加。我们确定了三个不同的高危险带,主要集中在已经温带的水库范围内:北美西部和中部、延伸到俄罗斯西部的欧洲中部和东北部以及南美洲中部境内的离散斑块。至关重要的是,我们的研究结果表明,汉坦病毒的危害从根本上是沿着病毒特定的生态轴形成的。虽然高温和降水异常主导了辛诺布雷病毒和泰国病毒等新旧世界谱系的传播动态,但首尔病毒和马莫雷病毒出现了独特的以城市为中心的特征,这明显是由精细尺度的人为景观改变驱动的。这些结果表明,气候变化不会简单地均匀加剧病毒溢出,而是会在不同的气候和人类主导的环境中从根本上重组病毒溢出。我们的地图为有针对性的全球监测提供了关键基线,并强调了将空间保护规划与公共卫生准备相结合的迫切需要。

Ecological Cascades and Future Hantavirus Spillover Risk in a Changing Climate

Authors: Pranav Pandit, Andie H. Jian, Ayushi Gupta, Pranav Kulkarni, Pandit PS, Jian AH, Gupta A, Kulkarni PS, Pranav S. Pandit, Pranav S. Kulkarni
Original Abstract
Abstract Anthropogenic climate change is reshuffling global biodiversity and accelerating zoonotic spillover at the human-wildlife interface. The 2026 multi-country cruise ship outbreak of Andes virus highlights the unpredictable and globalized threat posed by rodent-borne orthohantaviruses, which cause severe, highly fatal human diseases worldwide. Despite this significant public health risk, macroecological synthesis linking environmental drivers to systemic shifts in hantavirus hazards has been lacking. Here, we implement an integrated ecological and machine learning framework combining 89 unique virus-host associations across 61 reservoir species with mechanistic Force-of-Infection (FOI) modeling to project global spillover hazards under current and future climate scenarios (SSP2-4.5 and SSP5-8.5) for 2041–2060. Our models demonstrate a spatially uneven but geographically extensive expansion of spillover hazards, with approximately 74.9% of modeled land area experiencing an increase in FOI. We identify three distinct high-hazard belts heavily concentrated in already-temperate reservoir ranges: western and central North America, central and northeastern Europe extending into western Russia, and discrete patches within central South America. Crucially, our findings reveal that hantavirus hazards are fundamentally shaped along virus-specific ecological axes. While thermal and precipitation anomalies predominate the transmission dynamics of Old and New World lineages like Sin-Nombre virus and Thailand virus, distinct urban-centric Profiles emerge for Seoul virus and Mamore virus, driven explicitly by fine-scale anthropogenic landscape modifications. These results reveal that climate change will not simply intensify viral spillover uniformly but will fundamentally restructure it across varied climatic and human-dominated environments. Our maps provide a critical baseline for targeted global surveillance and highlight the urgent need to integrate spatial conservation planning with public health preparedness.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究 · E1

游轮上的汉坦病毒:COVID-19 教给我们的。

Hantavirus on a Cruise Ship: What COVID-19 Taught Us.
Authors: Meyerowitz EA, Richterman A, Eric A Meyerowitz, Aaron Richterman, Eric A. Meyerowitz
摘要中文翻译摘要:在 COVID-19 大流行之初,人们对气溶胶传播机制(SARS-CoV-2 传播的主要模式)的认识延迟,导致有关非药物干预措施的早期建议不足,以及有关如何降低感染风险的初始信息传递不佳。我们重点介绍了安第斯病毒 (ANDV) 在 MV Hondius 游轮上传播的关键流行病学细节,以及之前报告的阿根廷境内已确认的人际传播的疫情爆发期间的关键流行病学细节,这表明气溶胶传播可能是在允许的环境条件下源病例和易感接触者之间 ANDV 传播的关键机制。

Hantavirus on a Cruise Ship: What COVID-19 Taught Us.

Authors: Meyerowitz EA, Richterman A, Eric A Meyerowitz, Aaron Richterman, Eric A. Meyerowitz
Original Abstract
Abstract Delayed recognition of the mechanics of aerosol transmission – the primary mode of spread of SARS-CoV-2 – at the beginning of the COVID-19 pandemic led to inadequate early recommendations regarding nonpharmaceutical interventions and poor initial messaging about how to reduce infection risk. We highlight key epidemiological details of Andes virus (ANDV) transmission on the MV Hondius cruise ship and during prior reported outbreaks with confirmed person-to-person spread in Argentina, which, together, suggest aerosol spread may be a key mechanism of transmission of ANDV between source cases and susceptible contacts in permissible environmental settings.
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
摘要:在 COVID-19 大流行之初,人们对气溶胶传播机制(SARS-CoV-2 传播的主要模式)的认识延迟,导致有关非药物干预措施的早期建议不足,以及有关如何降低感染风险的初始信息传递不佳。

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究 · E2

急性普马拉汉坦病毒感染早期和晚期细胞因子的表达

Cytokine expression during early and late phase of acute Puumala hantavirus infection
Authors: Mahmoud Sadeghi, Isabella Eckerle, Volker Daniel, Ulrich Burkhardt, Gerhard Opelz, Paul Schnitzler
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 布尼亚病毒科的汉坦病毒是新兴的人畜共患病原体,在旧世界引起肾综合征出血热(HFRS),在新世界引起汉坦病毒肺综合征(HPS)。讨论了这两种综合征的免疫介导的发病机制。我们研究的目的是调查急性普马拉汉坦病毒感染过程中细胞因子的表达。结果 我们回顾性研究了 2010 年德国汉坦病毒流行期间因急性普马拉汉坦病毒感染住院的 64 名患者。抗汉坦病毒 IgG/IgM 阳性证实汉坦病毒感染。通过ELISA分析急性汉坦病毒感染早期和晚期(平均分别为症状出现后6天和12天)IL-2、IL-5、IL-6、IL-8、IL-10、IFN-γ、TNF-α和TGF-β1的细胞因子表达。详细描述了 2010 年德国汉坦病毒流行期间需要住院治疗的严重汉坦病毒感染的人口统计和临床表现。与健康对照相比,急性汉坦病毒感染的特征是早期和晚期 IL-2、IL-6、IL-8、TGF-β1 和 TNF-α 水平显着升高。从疾病早期到晚期,IL-6、IL-10和TNF-α显着降低,而TGF-β1水平升高。以肌酐升高和血小板计数低为特征的疾病严重程度与高促炎性 IL-6 和 TNF-α 水平相关,但与低免疫抑制性 TGF-β1 水平相关,反之亦然。结论 在疾病早期阶段激活 T 淋巴细胞、单核细胞和巨噬细胞的细胞因子的高表达支持了免疫介导的发病机制的假设。在疾病晚期,免疫抑制性TGF-β1水平显着升高。我们认为,延迟诱导保护性免疫机制来下调大量早期促炎性免疫反应可能会导致人类汉坦病毒感染的病理特征。

Cytokine expression during early and late phase of acute Puumala hantavirus infection

Authors: Mahmoud Sadeghi, Isabella Eckerle, Volker Daniel, Ulrich Burkhardt, Gerhard Opelz, Paul Schnitzler
Original Abstract
Abstract Background Hantaviruses of the family Bunyaviridae are emerging zoonotic pathogens which cause hemorrhagic fever with renal syndrome (HFRS) in the Old World and hantavirus pulmonary syndrome (HPS) in the New World. An immune-mediated pathogenesis is discussed for both syndromes. The aim of our study was to investigate cytokine expression during the course of acute Puumala hantavirus infection. Results We retrospectively studied 64 patients hospitalised with acute Puumala hantavirus infection in 2010 during a hantavirus epidemic in Germany. Hantavirus infection was confirmed by positive anti-hantavirus IgG/IgM. Cytokine expression of IL-2, IL-5, IL-6, IL-8, IL-10, IFN-γ, TNF-α and TGF-β1 was analysed by ELISA during the early and late phase of acute hantavirus infection (average 6 and 12 days after onset of symptoms, respectively). A detailed description of the demographic and clinical presentation of severe hantavirus infection requiring hospitalization during the 2010 hantavirus epidemic in Germany is given. Acute hantavirus infection was characterized by significantly elevated levels of IL-2, IL-6, IL-8, TGF-β1 and TNF-α in both early and late phase compared to healthy controls. From early to late phase of disease, IL-6, IL-10 and TNF-α significantly decreased whereas TGF-β1 levels increased. Disease severity characterized by elevated creatinine and low platelet counts was correlated with high pro-inflammatory IL-6 and TNF-α but low immunosuppressive TGF-β1 levels and vice versa . Conclusion High expression of cytokines activating T-lymphocytes, monocytes and macrophages in the early phase of disease supports the hypothesis of an immune-mediated pathogenesis. In the late phase of disease, immunosuppressive TGF-β1 level increase significantly. We suggest that delayed induction of a protective immune mechanism to downregulate a massive early pro-inflammatory immune response might contribute to the pathologies characteristic of human hantavirus infection.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究 · E1

利巴韦林可保护叙利亚仓鼠免受致命的汉坦病毒肺部综合症——鼻内暴露于安第斯病毒后

Ribavirin Protects Syrian Hamsters against Lethal Hantavirus Pulmonary Syndrome — After Intranasal Exposure to Andes Virus
Authors: Monica Ogg, Colleen Jonsson, Jeremy Camp, Jay Hooper
摘要中文翻译安第斯病毒 (ANDV) 由野生啮齿类动物携带,传播给人类后会引起高度致命的汉坦病毒肺综合征 (HPS),导致 30% 至 50% 的病例死亡。由于这种疾病尚无治疗方法,我们在体外和动物模型中系统地测试了利巴韦林的疗效。体外测定证实了抗病毒活性,并确定最有效的剂量为 40 μg/mL 及以上。我们测试了三种不同浓度的利巴韦林在 ANDV 仓鼠模型中经鼻内攻击后预防 HPS 的能力。虽然最高浓度的利巴韦林 (200 mg/kg) 对仓鼠有毒,但中间浓度 (100 mg/kg) 和最低浓度 (50 mg/kg) 均可预防仓鼠发生 HPS,且无毒性。具体来说,这两个组的 8 只仓鼠中有 8 只在鼻内攻击中幸存下来,而 8 只 PBS 对照处理的动物中有 7 只出现了致命的 HPS。此外,我们报告说,从感染后第 6、8、10 或 12 天开始,以 50 mg/kg/天的剂量给予利巴韦林,可对所有组中的 HPS 产生显着的保护作用。感染后 14 天给予利巴韦林也能提供显着水平的针对致命 HPS 的保护。这些数据提供了体内证据,支持利巴韦林作为暴露后治疗的潜在用途,以预防经呼吸道暴露后的 HPS。

Ribavirin Protects Syrian Hamsters against Lethal Hantavirus Pulmonary Syndrome — After Intranasal Exposure to Andes Virus

Authors: Monica Ogg, Colleen Jonsson, Jeremy Camp, Jay Hooper
Original Abstract
Andes virus, ANDV, harbored by wild rodents, causes the highly lethal hantavirus pulmonary syndrome (HPS) upon transmission to humans resulting in death in 30% to 50% of the cases. As there is no treatment for this disease, we systematically tested the efficacy of ribavirin in vitro and in an animal model. In vitro assays confirmed antiviral activity and determined that the most effective doses were 40 μg/mL and above. We tested three different concentrations of ribavirin for their capability to prevent HPS in the ANDV hamster model following an intranasal challenge. While the highest level of ribavirin (200 mg/kg) was toxic to the hamster, both the middle (100 mg/kg) and the lowest concentration (50 mg/kg) prevented HPS in hamsters without toxicity. Specifically, 8 of 8 hamsters survived intranasal challenge for both of those groups whereas 7 of 8 PBS control-treated animals developed lethal HPS. Further, we report that administration of ribavirin at 50 mg/kg/day starting on days 6, 8, 10, or 12 post-infection resulted in significant protection against HPS in all groups. Administration of ribavirin at 14 days post-infection also provided a significant level of protection against lethal HPS. These data provide in vivo evidence supporting the potential use of ribavirin as a post-exposure treatment to prevent HPS after exposure by the respiratory route.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究 · E2

1997-2017 年阿根廷西北部与气候变化相关的汉坦病毒肺综合征爆发

Hantavirus pulmonary syndrome outbreaks associated with climate variability in Northwestern Argentina, 1997–2017
Authors: Ignacio Ferro, Carla M. Bellomo, Walter López, Rocío Coelho, Daniel Alonso, Agostina Bruno, Francisco E. Córdoba, Valeria P. Martinez
摘要中文翻译背景 啮齿动物传播的汉坦病毒(正汉坦病毒属)是引起两种人类疾病的病原体:欧亚大陆的肾综合征出血热(HFRS);以及北美和南美的汉坦病毒肺综合征(HPS)。在南美洲,HPS 的死亡率可达 35%–50%。致病性汉坦病毒向人类传播主要是通过吸入受感染啮齿动物的雾化排泄物。因此,HPS 的流行病学必然与其啮齿动物宿主的生态以及与人类的接触有关,而这反过来又可能受到气候变化的影响。在这里,我们研究了气候变量与汉坦病毒传播之间的关系,旨在开发基于生态相关气候因素的潜在汉坦病毒爆发的早期预警系统。方法和主要发现 我们汇编了 1997 年至 2017 年期间阿根廷西北部报告的 HPS 病例,并将我们的数据分为半年、每季度和半月时间段,以便观察年度和较短的时间延迟。为了评估汉坦病毒传播与平均气温和降水的关系,我们使用了动态回归分析。我们发现 HPS 发生率与滞后降雨量和温度(延迟 2 至 6 个月)之间存在显着关联。对于每半年和每季度的模型,汉坦病毒传播与滞后的降雨量和温度呈正相关;而双季模型表明与降雨量有直接关系,但在第二个滞后期与温度成反比。结论/意义 这项工作表明,气候变化在阿根廷西北部汉坦病毒的传播中发挥着重要作用。本研究开发的模型为根据气候参数预测潜在 HPS 爆发提供了基础。我们的研究结果对于制定公共卫生政策和预防策略以减轻可能的疫情爆发具有重要意义。尽管如此,对啮齿动物种群动态的监测计划将有助于更准确地预测 HPS 的爆发。

Hantavirus pulmonary syndrome outbreaks associated with climate variability in Northwestern Argentina, 1997–2017

Authors: Ignacio Ferro, Carla M. Bellomo, Walter López, Rocío Coelho, Daniel Alonso, Agostina Bruno, Francisco E. Córdoba, Valeria P. Martinez
Original Abstract
Background Rodent-borne hantaviruses (genus Orthohantavirus ) are the etiologic agents causing two human diseases: hemorrhagic fever with renal syndrome (HFRS) in Euroasia; and hantavirus pulmonary syndrome (HPS) in North and South America. In South America fatality rates of HPS can reach up to 35%–50%. The transmission of pathogenic hantaviruses to humans occurs mainly via inhalation of aerosolized excreta from infected rodents. Thus, the epidemiology of HPS is necessarily linked to the ecology of their rodent hosts and the contact with a human, which in turn may be influenced by climatic variability. Here we examined the relationship between climatic variables and hantavirus transmission aim to develop an early warning system of potential hantavirus outbreaks based on ecologically relevant climatic factors. Methodology and main findings We compiled reported HPS cases in northwestern Argentina during the 1997–2017 period and divided our data into biannual, quarterly, and bimestrial time periods to allow annual and shorter time delays to be observed. To evaluate the relationship of hantavirus transmission with mean temperature and precipitation we used dynamic regression analysis. We found a significant association between HPS incidence and lagged rainfall and temperature with a delay of 2 to 6 months. For the biannual and quarterly models, hantavirus transmission was positively associated with lagged rainfall and temperature; whereas the bimestrial models indicate a direct relationship with the rainfall but inverse for temperature in the second lagged period. Conclusions/Significance This work demonstrates that climate variability plays a significant role in the transmission of hantavirus in northwestern Argentina. The model developed in this study provides a basis for the forecast of potential HPS outbreaks based on climatic parameters. Our findings are valuable for the development of public health policies and prevention strategies to mitigate possible outbreaks. Nonetheless, a surveillance program on rodent population dynamics would lead to a more accurate forecast of HPS outbreaks.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究 · E1

汉坦病毒肺综合征的流行病学描述、病死率和趋势:阿根廷 9 年监测

Epidemiological description, case‐fatality rate, and trends of Hantavirus Pulmonary Syndrome: 9 years of surveillance in Argentina
Authors: Daniel Oscar Alonso, Ayelen Iglesias, Rocio Coelho, Natalia Periolo, Agostina Bruno, Maria Teresa Córdoba, Noemi Filomarino, Marcelo Quipildor, Emiliano Biondo, Eduardo Fortunato
摘要中文翻译摘要 汉坦病毒肺综合征(HPS)是美洲受影响最严重的国家之一阿根廷的地方病。安第斯病毒 (ANDV) 是在阿根廷引起 HPS 的主要正汉坦病毒属。本研究分析了阿根廷所有流行地区 HPS 的地理分布、临床表现和流行病学特征。我们重点关注了汉坦病毒国家参比实验室在 2009 年至 2017 年期间确诊的 533 例 HPS 病例的临床和流行病学数据。病死率为 21.4%,大多数病例临床情况严重,需要重症监护治疗 (84%)。自 1995 年首次检测到 HPS 以来,病死率总体呈下降趋势。经过超过 22 年的 HPS 诊断和监测经验,我们讨论了与这一趋势相关的一些可能因素。这种临床和流行病学分析提供了全球视角,有助于发现趋势和模式,更新国家一级的预防措施,并评估其对公共卫生的影响。

Epidemiological description, case‐fatality rate, and trends of Hantavirus Pulmonary Syndrome: 9 years of surveillance in Argentina

Authors: Daniel Oscar Alonso, Ayelen Iglesias, Rocio Coelho, Natalia Periolo, Agostina Bruno, Maria Teresa Córdoba, Noemi Filomarino, Marcelo Quipildor, Emiliano Biondo, Eduardo Fortunato
Original Abstract
Abstract Hantavirus pulmonary syndrome (HPS) is an endemic disease in Argentina, one of the most affected countries in the Americas. Andes virus (ANDV) is the main Orthohantavirus species causing HPS in Argentina. In this study, the geographical distribution, clinical presentation, and epidemiological features of HPS from all endemic regions of Argentina were analyzed. We focused on the clinical and epidemiological data from 533 HPS cases confirmed during the period 2009 to 2017 by the National Reference Laboratory for Hantavirus. A case‐fatality rate of 21.4% was registered, and most of the cases presented a severe clinical picture requiring intensive care treatment (84%). Since HPS first detection in 1995 the case‐fatality rate showed a general trend towards a decrease. After more than 22 years of experience in HPS diagnosis and surveillance, we discuss some possible factors implicated in this tendency. This clinical and epidemiological analysis gives a global perspective, being useful to detect trends and patterns, to update preventive actions at a national level, and evaluate their impact on public health.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究 · E1

恒河猴汉坦病毒肺综合征的病理生理学

Pathophysiology of hantavirus pulmonary syndrome in rhesus macaques
Authors: David Safronetz, Joseph Prescott, Friederike Feldmann, Elaine Haddock, Rebecca Rosenke, Atsushi Okumura, Douglas Brining, Eric Dahlstrom, Stephen F. Porcella, Hideki Ebihara
摘要中文翻译意义 汉坦病毒肺综合征 (HPS) 是一种罕见但通常致命的疾病,由新世界汉坦病毒感染引起。缺乏实验疾病模型是了解 HPS 发病机制和制定针对该疾病的医学对策的限制。在这项研究中,我们描述了一种新型非人类灵长类动物 HPS 模型的表征。在感染仅由鹿鼠传代的 Sin Nombre 病毒后,猕猴出现了严重的呼吸道疾病(HPS)。病毒血症和血液学异常是随后发生的疾病的最早标志,与呼吸窘迫发作相关的通透性过高与仅肺内皮细胞的宿主反应失调相一致。该模型将有助于增进我们对 HPS 的理解和治疗策略的临床前开发。

Pathophysiology of hantavirus pulmonary syndrome in rhesus macaques

Authors: David Safronetz, Joseph Prescott, Friederike Feldmann, Elaine Haddock, Rebecca Rosenke, Atsushi Okumura, Douglas Brining, Eric Dahlstrom, Stephen F. Porcella, Hideki Ebihara
Original Abstract
Significance Hantavirus pulmonary syndrome (HPS) is a rare but often fatal disease caused by infection with New World hantaviruses. A limitation to understanding the pathogenesis of HPS and developing medical countermeasures against this disease is a lack of experimental disease models. In this study we describe the characterization of a novel nonhuman primate model of HPS. After infection with deer mouse-only–passaged Sin Nombre virus, macaques developed severe respiratory disease indicative of HPS. Viremia and hematological abnormalities were the earliest markers of ensuing disease, and the hyperpermeability associated with the onset of respiratory distress coincided with dysregulation of host responses exclusively in the pulmonary endothelium. This model will help advance our understanding of HPS and preclinical development of therapeutic strategies.

汉坦病毒肺综合征儿科患者休克和多器官衰竭的快速进展

Rapid Progression of Shock and Multi-Organ Failure in a Pediatric Patient With Hantavirus Pulmonary Syndrome
Authors: J. Patel, S.L. Martiniano
摘要中文翻译摘要:汉坦病毒肺综合征(HPS)是一种罕见但往往致命的人畜共患疾病,尤其在儿童患者中罕见。它的早期表现可以模仿其他病毒或肿瘤状况,使诊断变得困难。该病例强调了对一名出现未分化休克的 5 岁男性进行广泛诊断测试的重要性。描述:一名健康的 5 岁男性,因上呼吸道症状、结膜炎、发烧 2 天、吐血和黑便 3 周后,因不明原因休克入住儿科重症监护室。入院时,他出现心动过速、呼吸急促、急性低氧性呼吸衰竭,并伴有肝肿大和脾肿大。最初的实验室显示白细胞增多(86,000 个细胞/uL)、血小板减少、凝血障碍和炎症标志物升高。当时的鉴别诊断包括噬血细胞性淋巴组织细胞增多症、白血病以及对典型或非典型感染的严重类白血病反应。严重暴露包括生活在有多种动物(包括啮齿动物)的农场。患者被置于飞沫隔离中,并接受了液体复苏和补充氧气,但病情迅速恶化,入院7小时后需要插管和机械通气,随后发生低氧性心脏骤停和35分钟的心肺复苏,然后在12小时时进行静脉-动脉体外膜氧合。尽管采取了这些措施,多器官衰竭还是接踵而至,CT胸部扫描显示肺部几乎完全缺气、腹水和胸腔积液。神经学评估显示严重脑水肿伴脑疝。肺部检查进行了诊断性支气管镜检查,但由于死亡临近,家人拒绝了。第三天停止维持生命治疗。验尸后,他的特定汉坦病毒血清学结果显示 Sin Nombre 病毒呈阳性。确认后,公共卫生部门获悉并能够对那些可能暴露的人进行监测并指导护理。讨论:该病例是罕见的患有严重致命性 HPS 的儿科患者的病例。在一项纳入 719 名 HPS 患者的研究中,只有 3% 的患者年龄在 12 岁以下。该患者出现非特异性症状,包括上呼吸道感染和白细胞增多,这些症状与细菌、病毒和肿瘤疾病重叠。HPS 进展迅速,在患有未分化休克的儿科病例中,特别是在可能接触啮齿动物的地区,临床医生应对 HPS 保持高度怀疑。早期识别和及时干预,包括体外生命支持,已被证明可以改善结果,但死亡率仍然很高。该病例强调了在儿科休克鉴别诊断中考虑 HPS 的重要性,以便进行早期积极的支持治疗和暴露后监测。

Rapid Progression of Shock and Multi-Organ Failure in a Pediatric Patient With Hantavirus Pulmonary Syndrome

Authors: J. Patel, S.L. Martiniano
Original Abstract
Abstract Introduction: Hantavirus Pulmonary Syndrome (HPS) is a rare but often fatal zoonotic disease, especially uncommon in pediatric patients. Its early presentation can mimic other viral or oncological conditions, making diagnosis challenging. This case highlights the importance of broad diagnostic testing for a 5-year-old male who presented with undifferentiated shock. Description: A healthy 5-year-old male was admitted to the Pediatric Intensive Care Unit with undifferentiated shock after a three-week history of upper respiratory symptoms, conjunctivitis, two days of fever, and one day of hematemesis and melena. On admission, he was tachycardic, tachypneic, and in acute hypoxemic respiratory failure with hepatomegaly and splenomegaly. Initial labs showed leukocytosis (86,000 cells/uL), thrombocytopenia, coagulopathy, and elevated inflammatory markers. Differential at that time included hemophagocytic lymphohistiocytosis, leukemia, and severe leukemoid reaction to typical or atypical infections. Significant exposures included living on a farm with a wide variety of animals including rodents. The patient was placed in droplet isolation, and received fluid resuscitation and supplemental oxygen, but rapidly deteriorated, requiring intubation and mechanical ventilation seven hours following admission, followed by hypoxemic cardiac arrest and 35 minutes of cardiopulmonary resuscitation, and then veno-arterial extracorporeal membrane oxygenation at 12 hours. Despite these measures, multiorgan failure ensued, and a CT chest scan revealed near-complete non-aerated lungs, ascites, and pleural effusions. Neurological evaluation indicated severe cerebral edema with herniation. Pulmonary was consulted for diagnostic bronchoscopy, however, this was declined by the family due to impending death. Life-sustaining treatment was withdrawn on day three. Post-mortem, his specific hantavirus serologies returned positive for Sin Nombre virus. Following confirmation, the public health department was informed and was able to provide surveillance and instruct care to those potentially exposed. Discussion: This case exemplifies a rare case of a pediatric patient with severe, fatal HPS. In one study, which included 719 HPS patients, only 3% of them were under the age of 12. This patient presented with nonspecific symptoms, including upper respiratory infection and leukocytosis, which overlapped with bacterial, viral, and oncological conditions. HPS progresses rapidly, and in pediatric cases with undifferentiated shock, especially in regions with potential rodent exposure, clinicians should maintain a high index of suspicion for HPS. Early recognition and timely intervention, including extracorporeal life support, have been shown to improve outcomes, however, mortality remains high. This case emphasizes the importance of considering HPS in the differential diagnosis for pediatric shock to allow for early and aggressive supportive care and post-exposure monitoring.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究 · E2

2020-2022年法国里昂城市公园野生大鼠褐家鼠首尔病毒的进化和遗传特征

Evolution and genetic characterization of Seoul virus in wild rats Rattus norvegicus from an urban park in Lyon, France 2020–2022
Authors: Hussein Alburkat, Teemu Smura, Marie Bouilloud, Julien Pradel, Gwendoline Anfray, Karine Berthier, Lara Dutra, Anne Loiseau, Thanakorn Niamsap, Viktor Olander
摘要中文翻译背景 首尔病毒(SEOV)是一种主要由大鼠携带的正汉坦病毒。在人类中,它可能导致肾综合征出血热(HFRS)。其发病率可能被低估,并且考虑到城市地区的扩张,需要更好地了解大鼠群体中 SEOV 的循环。除了需要改进人类病例检测之外,我们还需要加深对 SEOV 传播所涉及的生态、流行病学和进化过程的理解。方法/主要发现 我们在一座大城市(法国里昂)的一个热门城市公园对褐家鼠的 SEOV 进行了全面的血清学和分子表征,为设计有关 SEOV 的监测策略提供了重要信息。我们于 2020 年至 2022 年在里昂市“La Tête d’Or”城市公园内对老鼠进行了采样。我们结合了老鼠群体遗传学、免疫荧光测定、SEOV 高通量测序(S、M 和 L 片段)和系统发育分析。我们发现里昂市内野鼠种群的结构较低。公园内只有一个采样点(2021 年建成的建筑)显示出较高的遗传分化,值得进一步关注。我们证实了该公园老鼠体内存在 SEOV,其血清阳性率很高(17.2%),并且与 2011 年在里昂市描述的菌株具有高度遗传相似性。结论/意义 这项研究证实了 SEOV 在一个受欢迎的城市公园中持续循环,那里存在 SEOV 传播给人类的风险。对该病毒进行监测可以提供有效的早期预警系统,并有助于准备基于风险的干预措施。当我们发现公园和城市其他地区的老鼠种群之间存在高基因流动时,我们主张在整个城市范围内进行 SEOV 监测。

Evolution and genetic characterization of Seoul virus in wild rats Rattus norvegicus from an urban park in Lyon, France 2020–2022

Authors: Hussein Alburkat, Teemu Smura, Marie Bouilloud, Julien Pradel, Gwendoline Anfray, Karine Berthier, Lara Dutra, Anne Loiseau, Thanakorn Niamsap, Viktor Olander
Original Abstract
Background Seoul virus (SEOV) is an orthohantavirus primarily carried by rats. In humans, it may cause hemorrhagic fever with renal syndrome (HFRS). Its incidence is likely underestimated and given the expansion of urban areas, a better knowledge of SEOV circulation in rat populations is called for. Beyond the need to improve human case detection, we need to deepen our comprehension of the ecological, epidemiological, and evolutionary processes involved in the transmission of SEOV. Methodology / Principal findings We performed a comprehensive serological and molecular characterization of SEOV in Rattus norvegicus in a popular urban park within a large city (Lyon, France) to provide essential information to design surveillance strategies regarding SEOV. We sampled rats within the urban park of ‘La Tête d’Or’ in Lyon city from 2020 to 2022. We combined rat population genetics, immunofluorescence assays, SEOV high-throughput sequencing (S, M, and L segments), and phylogenetic analyses. We found low structuring of wild rat populations within Lyon city. Only one sampling site within the park (building created in 2021) showed high genetic differentiation and deserves further attention. We confirmed the circulation of SEOV in rats from the park with high seroprevalence (17.2%) and high genetic similarity with the strain previously described in 2011 in Lyon city. Conclusion/Significance This study confirms the continuous circulation of SEOV in a popular urban park where the risk for SEOV transmission to humans is present. Implementing a surveillance of this virus could provide an efficient early warning system and help prepare risk-based interventions. As we reveal high gene flow between rat populations from the park and the rest of the city, we advocate for SEOV surveillance to be conducted at the scale of the entire city.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究 · E1

普马拉病毒肾综合征出血热的分子诊断

Molecular Diagnosis of Hemorrhagic Fever with Renal Syndrome Caused by Puumala Virus
Authors: Nina Lagerqvist, Åsa Hagström, Malin Lundahl, Elin Nilsson, Mikael Juremalm, Inger Larsson, Erik Alm, Göran Bucht, Clas Ahlm, Jonas Klingström
摘要中文翻译摘要 啮齿动物传播的汉坦病毒引起两种严重的急性疾病:欧亚大陆的肾综合征出血热(HFRS)和美洲的汉坦病毒肺综合征(HPS;也称为汉坦病毒心肺综合征 [HCPS])。普马拉病毒 (PUUV) 是欧洲 HFRS 最常见的病原体。目前的常规诊断方法基于血清学分析,可能会产生不确定的结果。汉坦病毒感染者在疾病早期会出现病毒血症;因此,病毒 RNA 基因组的检测可以成为现有血清学方法的宝贵补充。然而,PUUV的高基因组序列多样性阻碍了分子诊断的发展,目前还没有实时逆转录定量(RT)-PCR检测可用于HFRS的常规诊断。在这里,我们提出了一种新颖的 PUUV RT-PCR 检测方法。该检测方法经过验证,可用于对 2013 年至 2014 年冬季在瑞典收集的样本进行 HFRS 常规诊断。该检测方法可在症状出现后 8 天内收集的 98.7% 已确诊的临床 HFRS 样本中检测到 PUUV RNA。总之,这项研究表明,实时 RT-PCR 可以成为 HFRS 早期血清学检测的可靠替代方案。

Molecular Diagnosis of Hemorrhagic Fever with Renal Syndrome Caused by Puumala Virus

Authors: Nina Lagerqvist, Åsa Hagström, Malin Lundahl, Elin Nilsson, Mikael Juremalm, Inger Larsson, Erik Alm, Göran Bucht, Clas Ahlm, Jonas Klingström
Original Abstract
ABSTRACT Rodent-borne hantaviruses cause two severe acute diseases: hemorrhagic fever with renal syndrome (HFRS) in Eurasia, and hantavirus pulmonary syndrome (HPS; also called hantavirus cardiopulmonary syndrome [HCPS]) in the Americas. Puumala virus (PUUV) is the most common causative agent of HFRS in Europe. Current routine diagnostic methods are based on serological analyses and can yield inconclusive results. Hantavirus-infected patients are viremic during the early phase of disease; therefore, detection of viral RNA genomes can be a valuable complement to existing serological methods. However, the high genomic sequence diversity of PUUV has hampered the development of molecular diagnostics, and currently no real-time reverse transcription-quantitative (RT)-PCR assay is available for routine diagnosis of HFRS. Here, we present a novel PUUV RT-PCR assay. The assay was validated for routine diagnosis of HFRS on samples collected in Sweden during the winter season from 2013 to 2014. The assay allowed detection of PUUV RNA in 98.7% of confirmed clinical HFRS samples collected within 8 days after symptomatic onset. In summary, this study shows that real-time RT-PCR can be a reliable alternative to serological tests during the early phase of HFRS.

青少年致命的汉坦病毒肺综合征

Fatal Hantavirus Pulmonary Syndrome in an Adolescent
Authors: Ali S. Khan, Thomas G. Ksiazek, Sherif R. Zaki, Stuart T. Nichol, Pierre E. Rollin, Cj Peters, Rima F. Khabbaz, James E. Cheek, Larry A. Shireley, Stephen L. McDonough
摘要中文翻译汉坦病毒肺综合征 (HPS) 是最近公认的一种病毒性人畜共患病,其特征是发热前驱症状进展为严重的非心源性肺水肿。1-4 这种综合征由至少三种新描述的汉坦病毒引起:第一种是 Sin Nombre 病毒,是西南汉坦病毒,在 1993 年夏季引起呼吸衰竭爆发;另一种是西南部汉坦病毒,在 1993 年夏季引起呼吸衰竭爆发。第二个是黑溪运河病毒,在佛罗里达州引起了一例 HPS 病例;在路易斯安那州一名患者的肺组织中发现了第三种汉坦病毒。通过酶联免疫吸附测定 (ELISA) 血清学检测,针对异源和同源汉坦病毒抗原的免疫球蛋白 M (IgM) 滴度升高,福尔马林固定组织的免疫组织化学呈阳性,或逆转录聚合酶链反应 (RT-PCR) 扩增冷冻组织中的汉坦病毒核苷酸序列。

Fatal Hantavirus Pulmonary Syndrome in an Adolescent

Authors: Ali S. Khan, Thomas G. Ksiazek, Sherif R. Zaki, Stuart T. Nichol, Pierre E. Rollin, Cj Peters, Rima F. Khabbaz, James E. Cheek, Larry A. Shireley, Stephen L. McDonough
Original Abstract
Hantavirus pulmonary syndrome (HPS) is a recently recognized viral zoonosis characterized by a febrile prodrome progressing to severe noncardiogenic pulmonary edema.1-4 This syndrome is caused by at least three newly described hantaviruses: the first, Sin Nombre virus, is the Southwestern hantavirus that caused an outbreak of respiratory failure during the summer of 1993; the second, Black Creek Canal virus, caused a case of HPS in Florida; and the third hantavirus was identified in lung tissue from a patient in Louisiana. Diagnosis is by enzyme-linked immunosorbent assay (ELISA) serology with elevated immunoglobulin M (IgM) titers against heterologous and homologous hantaviral antigens, positive immunohistochemistry on formalin-fixed tissue, or reverse transcriptase-polymerase chain reaction (RT-PCR) amplification of hantaviral nucleotide sequence from frozen tissue.
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
汉坦病毒肺综合征 (HPS) 是最近公认的一种病毒性人畜共患病,其特征是发热前驱症状进展为严重的非心源性肺水肿。1-4 该综合征由至少三种新描述的汉坦病毒引起:第一种是 Sin Nombre 病毒,是西南汉坦病毒,在 1993 年夏季引起呼吸衰竭爆发。第二种是黑溪运河病毒,在佛罗里达州引起了一例 HPS 病例。在路易斯安那州一名患者的肺组织中发现了第三种汉坦病毒。
设计与对象
通过酶联免疫吸附测定 (ELISA) 血清学检测,针对异源和同源汉坦病毒抗原的免疫球蛋白 M (IgM) 滴度升高,福尔马林固定组织的免疫组织化学呈阳性,或逆转录聚合酶链反应 (RT-PCR) 扩增冷冻组织中的汉坦病毒核苷酸序列。

北达科他州青少年的汉坦病毒肺综合征

Hantavirus Pulmonary Syndrome in an Adolescent from North Dakota
Authors: Ashton D. Hall, Danielle Fayad, Mary A. Staat
摘要中文翻译汉坦病毒肺部综合征仍然是一种罕见但新出现的病毒性人畜共患病,可导致儿童和青少年呼吸衰竭和血流动力学不稳定。由于其病程急性进展且死亡率高,治疗汉坦病毒肺综合征的临床医生应了解其流行病学危险因素、临床综合征和影像学表现。我们报道了一名来自北达科他州的 14 岁女性汉坦病毒肺综合征病例,她在感染后幸存下来,没有留下后遗症。

Hantavirus Pulmonary Syndrome in an Adolescent from North Dakota

Authors: Ashton D. Hall, Danielle Fayad, Mary A. Staat
Original Abstract
Hantavirus pulmonary syndrome remains an uncommon yet emerging viral zoonosis that causes respiratory failure and hemodynamic instability in children and adolescents. Due to its acutely progressive course and high mortality rate, clinicians treating hantavirus pulmonary syndrome should understand its epidemiologic risk factors, clinical syndrome and radiographic findings. We present a case of hantavirus pulmonary syndrome in a 14-year-old female from North Dakota who survived the infection without sequelae.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究 · E1

汉坦病毒肺综合征:1993–2018

Hantavirus Pulmonary Syndrome: 1993–2018
Authors: Lauren Thorp, Lynne Fullerton, Amy Whitesell, Walter Dehority
摘要中文翻译目标 从 1993 年到 2018 年,39 个州报告了汉坦病毒感染,其中汉坦病毒肺综合征 (HPS) 是最常见和致命的表现。为了确定儿童和成人之间 HPS 表现的差异,考虑到 HPS 的非特异性临床特征,我们假设患有 HPS 的儿童将在病程后期被诊断出来。方法 这是对 1993 年至 2018 年全国 HPS 病例的临床和人口特征的评估。数据来自疾病控制和预防中心数据库和 1 个州卫生部门,占美国病例的 97%。我们使用非参数和参数分析比较了儿童(0至12岁)、青少年(13至18岁)和成人,并进行了额外的探索性分析,以确定与死亡率相关的临床变量。结果 719例HPS患者中,≤12岁22例(3.0%),13~18岁47例(6.5%),其余650例(90.4%)为成人。总死亡率为 35.4%,不同年龄组之间没有差异 (P = .8)。症状出现和死亡之间的时间因年龄组而异,儿童的中位生存时间为 2 天(四分位距 [IQR] 2 至 3),青少年为 4 天(IQR 3 至 5),成人为 5 天(IQR 4 至 8;P = .001)。平均最高血细胞比容和中位最高肌酐水平与 0 至 18 岁人群的死亡率显着相关,但与成人无关。结论 在代表美国最大规模 HPS 研究的数据集中,我们发现患有 HPS 的儿童比成人死亡更快,并且最高的血细胞比容和肌酐水平仅与 19 岁以下儿童的死亡相关。

Hantavirus Pulmonary Syndrome: 1993–2018

Authors: Lauren Thorp, Lynne Fullerton, Amy Whitesell, Walter Dehority
Original Abstract
OBJECTIVES From 1993 to 2018, hantavirus infections were reported in 39 states, with hantavirus pulmonary syndrome (HPS) as the most common and fatal manifestation. To identify differences in the presentation of HPS between children and adults, we hypothesized that children with HPS would be diagnosed later in their illness course given the nonspecific clinical features of HPS. METHODS This was an evaluation of the clinical and demographic characteristics of national HPS cases from 1993 to 2018. Data were from the Centers for Disease Control and Prevention database and 1 state department of health, comprising 97% of US cases. We compared children (0 to 12 years), adolescents (13 to 18 years), and adults using nonparametric and parametric analyses, with additional exploratory analyses to identify clinical variables associated with mortality. RESULTS Among 719 HPS patients, 22 (3.0%) were aged ≤12 years, 47 (6.5%) were 13 to 18 years old, and the remaining 650 (90.4%) were adults. Overall mortality was 35.4% and did not differ between age groups (P = .8). The time between symptom onset and death differed by age group, with children living a median of 2 days (interquartile range [IQR] 2 to 3), adolescents 4 days (IQR 3 to 5), and adults 5 days (IQR 4 to 8; P = .001). The mean highest hematocrit and median highest creatinine level were significantly associated with mortality in those 0 to 18 years old but not adults. CONCLUSIONS In our dataset representing the largest study of HPS in the United States, we found that children with HPS died more quickly than adults and that highest hematocrit and creatinine levels were associated with death only among those &lt;19 years old.

华南城市地区褐家鼠和种家鼠肝组织样本中首尔病毒的检测及基因鉴定

Detection and Genetic Characterization of Seoul Virus in Liver Tissue Samples From Rattus norvegicus and Rattus tanezumi in Urban Areas of Southern China
Authors: Wenqiao He, Jiaqi Fu, Yuqi Wen, Mingji Cheng, Yun Mo, Qing Chen
摘要中文翻译啮齿动物是汉坦病毒的重要宿主,已知肺和肾是这些病毒的首选器官。最近,在匈牙利和美国的野生啮齿动物以及荷兰的饲养大鼠的肝脏样本中检测到了汉坦病毒。然而,很少有研究在中国大鼠的肝脏中检测到汉坦病毒。在这项研究中,对来自中国南方城市地区捕获的褐家鼠和种氏鼠的肝脏样本中的汉坦病毒进行了研究。总共捕获了 461 只褐家鼠和 64 只褐家鼠。使用泛汉坦病毒 PCR 方法,在这些动物的肝脏、肺和血清样本中检测到了汉坦病毒。约7.43%的肝脏样本呈首尔病毒(SEOV)阳性。褐家鼠肝脏样本中SEOV检出率(8.24%)高于种虾肝样本(1.56%),表明褐家鼠在中国城市地区SEOV传播中起主导作用。三只褐家鼠的肝脏样本中含有 SEOV RNA,但肺部样本中没有,这表明肝脏可能是 SEOV 的靶器官之一。扩增了来自 R. tanezumi 的 S 和 M 片段的第一个完整 SEOV 蛋白编码序列 (CDS) 以及 L 片段的部分 CDS。还获得了褐家鼠 S、M 和 L 片段的几个完整和部分 CDS。从不同动物获得的SEOV序列高度相似,表明褐家鼠和种氏鼠之间存在SEOV跨物种传播潜力。

Detection and Genetic Characterization of Seoul Virus in Liver Tissue Samples From Rattus norvegicus and Rattus tanezumi in Urban Areas of Southern China

Authors: Wenqiao He, Jiaqi Fu, Yuqi Wen, Mingji Cheng, Yun Mo, Qing Chen
Original Abstract
Rodents are important hosts of hantaviruses, and lungs and kidneys are known to be the preferred organs of these viruses. Recently, hantaviruses were detected in liver samples from wild rodents in Hungary and the United States, and feeder rats in the Netherlands. However, few studies have detected hantaviruses in the liver of rats from China. In this study, hantaviruses were investigated in liver samples from R. norvegicus and R. tanezumi trapped in urban areas of southern China. A total of 461 R. norvegicus and 64 R. tanezumi were trapped. Using a pan-hantavirus PCR method, hantaviruses were detected in liver, lung, and serum samples from these animals. About 7.43% of liver samples were positive for Seoul virus (SEOV). The detection rate of SEOV in liver samples from R. norvegicus (8.24%) was higher than that from R. tanezumi (1.56%), suggesting the predominant role of R. norvegicus in the transmission of SEOV in urban areas of China. Three R. norvegicus had SEOV RNA in their liver samples but not in their lung samples, suggesting that the liver might be one of the targeted organs of SEOV. The first full SEOV protein-coding sequences (CDS) of the S and M segments, and partial CDS of the L segment from R. tanezumi were amplified. Several full and partial CDS of the S, M, and L segments from R. norvegicus were also obtained. The SEOV sequences obtained from different animals were highly similar, suggesting the cross-species transmission potential of SEOV between R. norvegicus and R. tanezumi .

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究 · E2

急性普马拉汉坦病毒感染的凝血障碍

Coagulopathy in Acute Puumala Hantavirus Infection
Authors: Sirpa Koskela, Satu Mäkelä, Tomas Strandin, Antti Vaheri, Tuula Outinen, Lotta Joutsi-Korhonen, Ilkka Pörsti, Jukka Mustonen, Outi Laine
摘要中文翻译普马拉汉坦病毒 (PUUV) 引起肾综合征出血热 (HFRS),也称为流行性肾病 (NE),主要流行于欧洲和俄罗斯。临床特征包括血小板计数低、凝血功能改变、内皮激活和急性肾损伤 (AKI)。据报道,凝血途径和炎症介质以及补体和激肽释放酶-激肽系统之间存在多种联系。出血症状通常很轻微。PUUV 感染的患者发生弥散性血管内凝血 (DIC) 和血栓形成的风险也会增加。

Coagulopathy in Acute Puumala Hantavirus Infection

Authors: Sirpa Koskela, Satu Mäkelä, Tomas Strandin, Antti Vaheri, Tuula Outinen, Lotta Joutsi-Korhonen, Ilkka Pörsti, Jukka Mustonen, Outi Laine
Original Abstract
Puumala hantavirus (PUUV) causes a hemorrhagic fever with renal syndrome (HFRS), also called nephropathia epidemica (NE), which is mainly endemic in Europe and Russia. The clinical features include a low platelet count, altered coagulation, endothelial activation, and acute kidney injury (AKI). Multiple connections between coagulation pathways and inflammatory mediators, as well as complement and kallikrein–kinin systems, have been reported. The bleeding symptoms are usually mild. PUUV-infected patients also have an increased risk for disseminated intravascular coagulation (DIC) and thrombosis.

汉坦病毒感染会导致成年实验小鼠致命的急性神经系统疾病

Hantaan Virus Infection Causes an Acute Neurological Disease That Is Fatal in Adult Laboratory Mice
Authors: Dominic Wichmann, Hermann-Josef Gröne, Michael Frese, Jovan Pavlovic, Bärbel Anheier, Otto Haller, Hans-Dieter Klenk, Heinz Feldmann
摘要中文翻译摘要 汉滩病毒是朝鲜出血热的病原体,通过持续感染的小鼠(黑线姬鼠)传播给人类,而黑线姬鼠是主要储存宿主。在这里,我们证明了成年小家鼠(C57BL/6、BALB/c、AKR/J 和 SJL/J)的几种品系在腹膜内感染时容易受到汉坦病毒感染。最初的临床症状是体重减轻、皮毛皱起和活动减少,随后出现神经系统症状,如瘫痪和抽搐。发病2天内,动物死于急性脑炎。PCR 分析表明所有主要器官均存在病毒 RNA 的全身感染。对死后材料进行免疫组织化学和原位杂交分析,检测到中枢神经系统(主要是大脑)、肝脏和脾脏中的病毒抗原和 RNA。在中枢神经系统中,病毒抗原和 RNA 与血管周围浸润共存,这是主要的病理发现。为了研究干扰素系统在汉坦病毒发病机制中的参与,我们感染了α/β干扰素受体敲除小鼠。这些动物更容易受到汉滩病毒感染,表明干扰素诱导的抗病毒防御机制在汉滩病毒发病机制中发挥着重要作用。本模型可能有助于克服针对汉坦病毒感染的治疗和预防措施开发中的缺点。

Hantaan Virus Infection Causes an Acute Neurological Disease That Is Fatal in Adult Laboratory Mice

Authors: Dominic Wichmann, Hermann-Josef Gröne, Michael Frese, Jovan Pavlovic, Bärbel Anheier, Otto Haller, Hans-Dieter Klenk, Heinz Feldmann
Original Abstract
ABSTRACT Hantaan virus, the etiological agent of Korean hemorrhagic fever, is transmitted to humans from persistently infected mice ( Apodemus agrarius ), which serve as the primary reservoir. Here we demonstrate that several strains of adult Mus musculus domesticus (C57BL/6, BALB/c, AKR/J, and SJL/J) were susceptible to Hantaan virus infection when infected intraperitoneally. First clinical signs were loss of weight, ruffled fur, and reduced activity, which were followed by neurological symptoms, such as paralyses and convulsions. Within 2 days of disease onset, the animals died of acute encephalitis. PCR analysis indicated a systemic infection with viral RNA present in all major organs. Immunohistochemical and in situ hybridization analyses of postmortem material detected viral antigen and RNA in the central nervous system (predominantly brain), liver, and spleen. In the central nervous system, viral antigen and RNA colocalized with perivascular infiltrations, the predominant pathological finding. To investigate the involvement of the interferon system in Hantaan virus pathogenesis, we infected alpha/beta interferon receptor knockout mice. These animals were more susceptible to Hantaan virus infection, indicating an important role of interferon-induced antiviral defense mechanisms in Hantaan virus pathogenesis. The present model may help to overcome shortcomings in the development of therapeutic and prophylactic measurements against hantavirus infections.

误诊为登革热的致命汉坦病毒肺综合征:对巴西里约热内卢州首例报告病例的调查

A Fatal Hantavirus Pulmonary Syndrome Misdiagnosed as Dengue: An Investigation into the First Reported Case in Rio de Janeiro State, Brazil
Authors: Renata Carvalho de Oliveira, Alexandro Guterres, Bernardo Rodrigues Teixeira, Jorlan Fernandes, João Marcos Penna Júnior, Reynaldo de Jesus Oliveira Júnior, Liana Strecht Pereira, João Bosco Júnior, Patrícia Soares Meneguete, Cristina Maria Giordano Dias
摘要中文翻译我们报告了巴西里约热内卢州一例汉坦病毒肺综合征 (HPS) 致命病例的调查结果,该州在 2015 年之前从未报告过该疾病。在接到 HPS 病例通报后,从该 HPS 患者的家庭成员和工作接触者中采集了血清样本,并检测了汉坦病毒抗体。在测试的 45 个人类样本中,汉坦病毒的血清阳性率为 22% (10/45)。在现场工作期间从 72 只啮齿动物采集了血液和组织样本,通过使用酶联免疫吸附测定 IgG 来评估汉坦病毒感染的流行情况,并通过逆转录聚合酶链反应和测序来表征啮齿动物汉坦病毒储库。抗体流行率为 6.9%。通过对S片段的核苷酸序列的分析,显示了两种啮齿动物——黑足侏儒鼠(Oligoryzomys nigripes)和光标草鼠(Akodoncursor)携带的单一基因型Juquitiba汉坦病毒的传播。Juquitiba 汉坦病毒在多种啮齿动物中传播,但主要在黑足侏儒鼠中传播。HPS 是里约热内卢州新认可的临床实体,患有发热性疾病和急性呼吸窘迫的患者应考虑治疗。

A Fatal Hantavirus Pulmonary Syndrome Misdiagnosed as Dengue: An Investigation into the First Reported Case in Rio de Janeiro State, Brazil

Authors: Renata Carvalho de Oliveira, Alexandro Guterres, Bernardo Rodrigues Teixeira, Jorlan Fernandes, João Marcos Penna Júnior, Reynaldo de Jesus Oliveira Júnior, Liana Strecht Pereira, João Bosco Júnior, Patrícia Soares Meneguete, Cristina Maria Giordano Dias
Original Abstract
We report the results of an investigation into a fatal case of hantavirus pulmonary syndrome (HPS) in Rio de Janeiro State, Brazil, where the disease had not been reported previous to 2015. Following the notification of an HPS case, serum samples were collected from the household members and work contacts of the HPS patient and tested for antibody to hantaviruses. Seroprevalence of 22% (10/45) was indicated for hantavirus out of 45 human samples tested. Blood and tissue samples were collected from 72 rodents during fieldwork to evaluate the prevalence of hantavirus infection, by using enzyme-linked immunosorbent assay IgG, and to characterize the rodent hantavirus reservoir(s), by reverse transcription polymerase chain reaction and sequencing. Antibody prevalence was 6.9%. The circulation of a single genotype, the Juquitiba hantavirus, carried by two rodent species, black-footed pigmy rice rat ( Oligoryzomys nigripes ) and cursor grass mouse ( Akodon cursor ), was shown by analysis of the nucleotide sequences of the S segment. Juquitiba hantavirus circulates in rodents of various species, but mainly in the black-footed pigmy rice rat. HPS is a newly recognized clinical entity in Rio de Janeiro State and should be considered in patients with febrile illness and acute respiratory distress.

宏基因组二代测序诊断首尔病毒感染相关颅内出血罕见病例

Rare case of intracranial hemorrhage associated with seoul virus infection diagnosed by metagenomic next‐generation sequencing
Authors: Dan Xie, Wen Xu, Ying Xian, Xiaofeng Yuan, Zhenchao Huang, Jingya You, Xiaogang Bi
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 首尔病毒(SEOV)是汉坦病毒属的一种,也是肾综合征出血热(HFRS)的病原体。诊断 SEOV 感染很困难,因为临床表现通常与其他病毒或细菌感染难以区分。此外,包括血清学和分子测定在内的诊断工具在临床环境中并不容易获得。病例报告 一名 57 岁男性,于 2019 年 11 月出现发烧和突然意识丧失。计算机断层扫描 (CT) 扫描显示硬膜下血肿、大脑镰下疝和脑梗死。他出现血小板减少症和转氨酶升高,但没有皮疹或明显的肾脏损害。他报告说自己被老鼠咬了。怀疑有 HFRS。汉坦病毒 IgG 呈阳性,宏基因组下一代测序 (mNGS) 直接在血液中检测到 SEOV 序列。结论 本报告强调了尽管没有皮肤和肾脏综合征出血表现,但对于伴有血小板减少和肝酶升高的发热患者怀疑 SEOV 感染的重要性。下一代测序是病原体检测的强大工具。作为 HFRS 肾外表现的颅内出血和脑梗塞很少见,但如本例所示是可能的。

Rare case of intracranial hemorrhage associated with seoul virus infection diagnosed by metagenomic next‐generation sequencing

Authors: Dan Xie, Wen Xu, Ying Xian, Xiaofeng Yuan, Zhenchao Huang, Jingya You, Xiaogang Bi
Original Abstract
Abstract Background Seoul virus (SEOV) is a Hantavirus and the causative pathogen of Hemorrhagic Fever with Renal Syndrome (HFRS). Diagnosing SEOV infection is difficult because the clinical presentations are often undistinguishable from other viral or bacterial infections. In addition, diagnostic tools including serological and molecular assays are not readily available in the clinical settings. Case Report A 57‐year‐old male presented with fever and a sudden loss of consciousness in November 2019. Computed tomography (CT) scan showed subdural hematoma, subfalcine herniation, and brain infarction. He developed thrombocytopenia and elevated transaminases, but no rashes or obvious kidney damage. He reported having a rat bite. HFRS was suspected. The Hantavirus IgG was positive, and the metagenomic next‐generation sequencing (mNGS) detected SEOV sequences directly in the blood. Conclusion This report highlights the importance of suspecting SEOV infection in febrile patients with thrombocytopenia and elevated liver enzymes despite the absence of hemorrhagic manifestations of skin and renal syndromes. Next‐generation sequencing is a powerful tool for pathogen detection. Intracranial hemorrhage and brain infarction as extrarenal manifestations of HFRS are rare but possible as demonstrated in this case.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究 · E1

有关淡黄小鼠(啮齿动物:仓鼠科)活动范围和活动的新数据有助于了解导致汉坦病毒肺综合征的安第斯病毒的传播和传播

New data about home range and movements of Oligoryzomys flavescens (Rodentia: Cricetidae) help to understand the spread and transmission of Andes virus that causes Hantavirus Pulmonary Syndrome
Authors: Malena Maroli, Belén Crosignani, Carlos I. Piña, Rocío Coelho, Valeria P. Martínez, Isabel Elisa Gómez Villafañe
摘要中文翻译摘要 汉坦病毒肺综合征是由正汉坦病毒属病毒引起的一种新发传染病。啮齿动物淡黄小鼠分布于南美洲四个国家。在阿根廷,苦参是 ANDV 正汉坦病毒三种基因型的储存库。这项工作的目的是通过线轴和无线电遥测技术来估计受感染和未受感染的苦参的活动范围大小和运动,以了解病毒的传播和传播。苦参的活动范围很广,春季时活动范围可达 1.82 公顷。正汉坦病毒感染并没有改变个体的行为。我们观察到感染者和未感染者的家庭范围有很大重叠,导致病毒在啮齿动物群体中传播的可能性很高。这些结果表明,岛屿环境中的人类健康风险可能很高,有关苦参运动生态的知识为预防提供了有用的信息。

New data about home range and movements of Oligoryzomys flavescens (Rodentia: Cricetidae) help to understand the spread and transmission of Andes virus that causes Hantavirus Pulmonary Syndrome

Authors: Malena Maroli, Belén Crosignani, Carlos I. Piña, Rocío Coelho, Valeria P. Martínez, Isabel Elisa Gómez Villafañe
Original Abstract
Abstract Hantavirus pulmonary syndrome is an emerging infectious disease caused by viruses of the genus Orthohantavirus . The rodent Oligoryzomys flavescens is distributed along four countries of South America. In Argentina, O. flavescens acts as a reservoir of three genotypes of ANDV orthohantavirus. The aims of this work were to estimate home range size and movements—with spool‐and‐line and radiotelemetry—of infected and non‐infected O. flavescens in order to understand the spread and transmission of the virus. O. flavescens use a wide area to satisfice its requirements, reaching a home range of 1.82 ha during spring. Orthohantavirus infection did not change the behaviour of individuals. We observed a great overlapping in the home range of infected and non‐infected individuals resulting in a high probability of virus dispersion on rodent population. These results show that human health risks could be high on island environments and knowledge about the movement ecology of O. flavescens provides useful information on prevention.

加拿大艾伯塔省出现多起汉坦病毒肺综合征病例

Cluster of Cases of Hantavirus Pulmonary Syndrome in Alberta, Canada
Authors: Duncan Webster, Bonita Lee, Ari Joffe, Wendy Sligl, Daryl Dick, Allen Grolla, Heinz Feldmann, Wadieh Yacoub, Karen Grimsrud, David Safronetz
摘要中文翻译2005年5月,加拿大阿尔伯塔省确诊了四例汉坦病毒肺综合征(HPS)病例。鉴于三起病例来自一个家庭并涉及一名 7 岁的儿童,这一聚集性病例很不寻常。这是加拿大报告的第一个家庭聚集性病例,也是北美报告的最年轻的 HPS 病例之一。

Cluster of Cases of Hantavirus Pulmonary Syndrome in Alberta, Canada

Authors: Duncan Webster, Bonita Lee, Ari Joffe, Wendy Sligl, Daryl Dick, Allen Grolla, Heinz Feldmann, Wadieh Yacoub, Karen Grimsrud, David Safronetz
Original Abstract
In May 2005, a cluster of four hantavirus pulmonary syndrome (HPS) cases was confirmed in Alberta, Canada. The cluster is unusual given that three cases were from a single family and involved a 7-year-old child. This is the first family cluster reported in Canada and includes one of the youngest cases of HPS reported in North America.

八名汉坦病毒肺综合征患者的高分辨率计算机断层扫描结果

High-resolution computed tomography findings in eight patients with hantavirus pulmonary syndrome
Authors: Diego de Lacerda Barbosa, Bruno Hochhegger, Arthur Soares Souza Jr., Gláucia Zanetti, Dante Luiz Escuissato, Gustavo de Souza Portes Meirelles, Marcelo Buarque de Gusmão Funari, Edson Marchiori
摘要中文翻译摘要 目的:本研究的目的是描述汉坦病毒肺综合征(HPS)患者的高分辨率计算机断层扫描(HRCT)结果。材料和方法:我们回顾性分析了 8 例 HPS 病例的 HRCT 结果。所有患者均为男性,年龄 19-70 岁(平均 41.7 岁)。所有患者均通过血清学检测(酶联免疫吸附测定)进行诊断。两名胸部放射科医生分析了图像并一致做出决定。结果:HRCT 的主要表现是毛玻璃样混浊 (GGO) 以及小叶间和小叶内平滑增厚,在所有 8 例病例中均发现;然而,这种疯狂铺路模式只在三个案例中被发现。5 名患者观察到胸腔积液和支气管血管周围增厚。所有患者的异常都是双侧的。结论:HPS 患者的主要 HRCT 表现是 GGO 和光滑的小叶间和小叶内间隔增厚,这可能与肺水肿的组织病理学表现相关。

High-resolution computed tomography findings in eight patients with hantavirus pulmonary syndrome

Authors: Diego de Lacerda Barbosa, Bruno Hochhegger, Arthur Soares Souza Jr., Gláucia Zanetti, Dante Luiz Escuissato, Gustavo de Souza Portes Meirelles, Marcelo Buarque de Gusmão Funari, Edson Marchiori
Original Abstract
Abstract Objective: The purpose of this study was to describe the high-resolution computed tomography (HRCT) findings in patients with hantavirus pulmonary syndrome (HPS). Materials and Methods: We retrospectively reviewed HRCT findings from eight cases of HPS. All patients were men, aged 19-70 (mean, 41.7) years. Diagnoses were established by serological test (enzyme-linked immunosorbent assay) in all patients. Two chest radiologists analyzed the images and reached decisions by consensus. Results: The predominant HRCT findings were ground-glass opacities (GGOs) and smooth inter- and intralobular septal thickening, found in all eight cases; however, the crazy-paving pattern was found in only three cases. Pleural effusion and peribronchovascular thickening were observed in five patients. The abnormalities were bilateral in all patients. Conclusion: The predominant HRCT findings in patients with HPS were GGOs and smooth inter- and intralobular septal thickening, which probably correlate with the histopathologic findings of pulmonary edema.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究 · E2

巴西汉坦病毒心肺综合征传播风险的时空动态

Spatiotemporal Dynamics of Hantavirus Cardiopulmonary Syndrome Transmission Risk in Brazil
Authors: Renata Muylaert, Gilberto Sabino-Santos, Paula Prist, Júlia Oshima, Bernardo Niebuhr, Thadeu Sobral-Souza, Stefan Oliveira, Ricardo Bovendorp, Jonathan Marshall, David Hayman
摘要中文翻译背景:人类汉坦病毒病很少见,但在新热带地区常常致命。几种数量丰富且分布广泛的乙齿鼠亚科啮齿动物是正汉坦病毒的主要宿主,与其他因素结合,这些啮齿动物可以形成汉坦病毒病。在这里,我们评估了 24 年来宿主多样性、气候、社会脆弱性和土地利用变化对巴西汉坦病毒病风险的影响。方法:在时空模型中使用景观变量(原生森林、林业、甘蔗、玉米和牧场)、气候(温度和降水)和宿主生物多样性(通过生态位模型得出),并使用 5570 个巴西城市作为分析单位。结果:原生林和甘蔗的数量以及温度是影响疾病风险增加的最重要因素。面临风险的人群(农村工人)和啮齿动物宿主多样性也对疾病风险产生积极影响。结论:土地利用变化,尤其是原住民地区转变为甘蔗田,可能对汉坦病毒病风险产生重大影响,可能是通过促进人与受感染啮齿动物之间的互动来实现的。我们的结果证明了了解景观变化、啮齿动物多样性和汉坦病毒疾病发病率之间相互作用的重要性,并建议土地利用政策应考虑疾病风险。同时,我们的风险地图可用于帮助分配预防措施以避免疾病。

Spatiotemporal Dynamics of Hantavirus Cardiopulmonary Syndrome Transmission Risk in Brazil

Authors: Renata Muylaert, Gilberto Sabino-Santos, Paula Prist, Júlia Oshima, Bernardo Niebuhr, Thadeu Sobral-Souza, Stefan Oliveira, Ricardo Bovendorp, Jonathan Marshall, David Hayman
Original Abstract
Background: Hantavirus disease in humans is rare but frequently lethal in the Neotropics. Several abundant and widely distributed Sigmodontinae rodents are the primary hosts of Orthohantavirus and, in combination with other factors, these rodents can shape hantavirus disease. Here, we assessed the influence of host diversity, climate, social vulnerability and land use change on the risk of hantavirus disease in Brazil over 24 years. Methods: Landscape variables (native forest, forestry, sugarcane, maize and pasture), climate (temperature and precipitation), and host biodiversity (derived through niche models) were used in spatiotemporal models, using the 5570 Brazilian municipalities as units of analysis. Results: Amounts of native forest and sugarcane, combined with temperature, were the most important factors influencing the increase of disease risk. Population at risk (rural workers) and rodent host diversity also had a positive effect on disease risk. Conclusions: Land use change—especially the conversion of native areas to sugarcane fields—can have a significant impact on hantavirus disease risk, likely by promoting the interaction between the people and the infected rodents. Our results demonstrate the importance of understanding the interactions between landscape change, rodent diversity, and hantavirus disease incidence, and suggest that land use policy should consider disease risk. Meanwhile, our risk map can be used to help allocate preventive measures to avoid disease.

藻类凝集素格里菲辛在体外和体内抑制汉滩病毒感染

An algal lectin griffithsin inhibits Hantaan virus infection in vitro and in vivo
Authors: Yajing Zhao, Ningbo Zhao, Yanxing Cai, Hui Zhang, Jia Li, Jiaqi Liu, Chuantao Ye, Yuan Wang, Yamei Dang, Wanying Li
摘要中文翻译汉坦病毒(HTNV)是东亚地区肾综合征出血热的病原体。目前还没有批准治疗 HTNV 和其他汉坦病毒感染的有效疗法。我们发现 Griffithsin (GRFT) 是一种藻类凝集素,对多种包膜病毒具有广谱抗病毒活性,可以抑制 HTNV 的生长和传播。使用重组水泡性口炎病毒(rVSV)和HTNV糖蛋白作为模型的体外实验表明,GRFT抑制rVSV-HTNV-G进入宿主细胞。此外,我们证明 GRFT 通过与病毒 N -聚糖结合,在体外预防真正的 HTNV 感染。体内实验表明,GRFT 部分保护乳鼠免受颅内暴露于 HTNV 引起的死亡。这些结果表明 GRFT 可以成为抑制 HTNV 感染的有前途的药物。

An algal lectin griffithsin inhibits Hantaan virus infection in vitro and in vivo

Authors: Yajing Zhao, Ningbo Zhao, Yanxing Cai, Hui Zhang, Jia Li, Jiaqi Liu, Chuantao Ye, Yuan Wang, Yamei Dang, Wanying Li
Original Abstract
Hantaan virus (HTNV) is the etiological pathogen of hemorrhagic fever with renal syndrome in East Asia. There are currently no effective therapeutics approved for HTNV and other hantavirus infections. We found that griffithsin (GRFT), an algae-derived lectin with broad-spectrum antiviral activity against various enveloped viruses, can inhibit the growth and spread of HTNV. In vitro experiments using recombinant vesicular stomatitis virus (rVSV) with HTNV glycoproteins as a model revealed that the GRFT inhibited the entry of rVSV-HTNV-G into host cells. In addition, we demonstrated that GRFT prevented authentic HTNV infection in vitro by binding to the viral N -glycans. In vivo experiments showed that GRFT partially protected the suckling mice from death induced by intracranial exposure to HTNV. These results demonstrated that GRFT can be a promising agent for inhibiting HTNV infection.

汉滩病毒所致高淀粉酶血症:西部地区101例肾综合征出血热患者的回顾性研究

Hyperamylasemia Caused by the Hantaan Virus: A Retrospective Study of 101 Patients with Hemorrhagic Fever with Renal Syndrome in West China
Authors: Sumei Zhang, Lei Shi, Xiaoqian Jiang, Xiaoling Yang, Libo Wu, Chuan Wang, Fuchun Jing, Tianyan Chen
摘要中文翻译背景。高淀粉酶血症(HA)是中国西部宝鸡市肾综合征出血热(HFRS)的一种不明显的表现。汉坦病毒(HTNV)是该地区唯一引起HFRS的病原体,但对当地HFRS患者HA的了解有限。本研究的目的是探讨HA的特点及其预测危险因素,以便医生在疾病早期及时监测和处理HA预警的可能的严重变化,以改善最终的结局。方法。所有伴和不伴 HA 的 HFRS 患者(分别为 HA 组和 nHA 组)均在宝鸡市人民医院接受治疗。通过Student's t检验或卡方检验比较两组之间的临床特征。通过逻辑回归分析测量预后的危险因素。通过受试者工作特征曲线分析临床和实验室参数对预后的预​​测作用。结果。46.53%的患者表现出HA,其中71.7%为重型和危重型HFRS,高于nHA组(19.57%,P < 0.001)。HA 组住院天数和急性胰腺炎(AP)总发生率均长于或高于 nHA 组( P < 0.01)。年龄和从首次症状出现到患者入院的时间(TOA)是HA的预测危险因素。最佳截止值为 54 岁,TOA 为 5.5 天。结论。HTNV 诱发的 HA 是中国西部地区 HFRS 患者常见的临床表现。它可以增加 HFRS 的严重程度、患者的住院天数以及 AP 的发生率。年龄和TOA是HTNV引起HA的独立危险因素。

Hyperamylasemia Caused by the Hantaan Virus: A Retrospective Study of 101 Patients with Hemorrhagic Fever with Renal Syndrome in West China

Authors: Sumei Zhang, Lei Shi, Xiaoqian Jiang, Xiaoling Yang, Libo Wu, Chuan Wang, Fuchun Jing, Tianyan Chen
Original Abstract
Background . Hyperamylasemia (HA) is an inconspicuous manifestation of hemorrhagic fever with renal syndrome (HFRS) in Baoji city, West China. Hantaan virus (HTNV) is the only pathogen‐caused HFRS in this region, but the knowledge about HA in the local HFRS patients has been limited. The aim of this study was to investigate the characteristics of HA and its predictive risk factors for doctors to engage in timely monitoring and dealing with the possible serious changes prewarned by HA in the early stages of the disease to improve the final outcome. Methods . All HFRS patients with and without HA (HA and nHA groups, respectively) were treated in Baoji People’s Hospital. The clinical characteristics between the two groups were compared by Student’s t ‐test or Chi‐square test. The risk factors for prognosis were measured by the logistic regression analysis. The predictive effects of prognosis in clinical and laboratory parameters were analyzed by the receiver operating characteristic curves. Results . 46.53% of the patients demonstrated HA, among which 71.7% were severe and critical types of HFRS, greater than that in the nHA group (19.57%, P < 0.001). The hospitalization day and the general incidence of acute pancreatitis (AP) were longer or greater in the HA group than in the nHA group ( P < 0.01). Age and the time from the onset of the first symptom to the patient being admitted to hospital ( T OA ) were the predictive risk factors for HA. The best cut‐off values were the age of 54 years and T OA of 5.5 days. Conclusion . HTNV‐induced HA is a common clinical presentation of HFRS patients in West China. It can increase the severity, the hospitalization days of patients, and the incidence of AP in HFRS. Age and T OA constituted independent risk factors for HA caused by HTNV.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究 · E2

活性汉坦病毒聚合酶的结构揭示了汉坦病毒科基因组复制的机制

Structures of active Hantaan virus polymerase uncover the mechanisms of Hantaviridae genome replication
Authors: Quentin Durieux Trouilleton, Sergio Barata-García, Benoît Arragain, Juan Reguera, Hélène Malet
摘要中文翻译摘要 汉坦病毒正在引起人类危及生命的人畜共患感染。它们的三联负链 RNA 基因组由多功能病毒 RNA 依赖性 RNA 聚合酶复制。在这里,我们描述了汉坦病毒聚合酶核心的结构并建立了体外复制活性的条件。apo 结构采用非活性构象,涉及聚合酶基序的大量折叠重排。5'病毒RNA启动子的结合触发汉坦病毒聚合酶重组和激活。它诱导 3' 病毒 RNA 向聚合酶活性位点募集,以启动启动和重新排列。延伸结构揭示了活性位点空腔中模板/产物双链体的形成,同时聚合酶核心加宽和 3' 病毒 RNA 二级结合位点的打开。总而言之,这些元素揭示了汉坦病毒科聚合酶结构的分子特异性,并揭示了复制的机制。它们为未来开发针对这组新兴病原体的抗病毒药物提供了坚实的框架。

Structures of active Hantaan virus polymerase uncover the mechanisms of Hantaviridae genome replication

Authors: Quentin Durieux Trouilleton, Sergio Barata-García, Benoît Arragain, Juan Reguera, Hélène Malet
Original Abstract
Abstract Hantaviruses are causing life-threatening zoonotic infections in humans. Their tripartite negative-stranded RNA genome is replicated by the multi-functional viral RNA-dependent RNA-polymerase. Here we describe the structure of the Hantaan virus polymerase core and establish conditions for in vitro replication activity. The apo structure adopts an inactive conformation that involves substantial folding rearrangement of polymerase motifs. Binding of the 5′ viral RNA promoter triggers Hantaan virus polymerase reorganization and activation. It induces the recruitment of the 3′ viral RNA towards the polymerase active site for prime-and-realign initiation. The elongation structure reveals the formation of a template/product duplex in the active site cavity concomitant with polymerase core widening and the opening of a 3′ viral RNA secondary binding site. Altogether, these elements reveal the molecular specificities of Hantaviridae polymerase structure and uncover the mechanisms underlying replication. They provide a solid framework for future development of antivirals against this group of emerging pathogens.

与阿根廷北部汉坦病毒肺综合征相关的新型正汉坦病毒

Novel Orthohantavirus Associated with Hantavirus Pulmonary Syndrome in Northern Argentina
Authors: Carla M. Bellomo, Sebastian Kehl, Daniel Oscar Alonso, Walter López, Flavia Cassinelli, Rocío María Coelho, Gabriela Bravo, Sara Aguirre, Marcela Dib, Natalia Periolo
摘要中文翻译在这项工作中,我们对阿根廷西北部已知流行区之外的汉坦病毒肺综合征致命病例相关的正汉坦病毒新变种进行了遗传特征分析。我们首先通过 ELISA、IgM 和 IgG 抗体检测确认了正汉坦病毒感染。然后,我们从 100 微升血清(唯一可用的样本)中提取 RNA,然后进行 RT-PCR。使用桑格和下一代测序技术对扩增子进行测序。我们从 S、M 和 L 区段分别获得了 1253 bp、799 bp 和 1675 bp 的部分序列,与所有先前表征的汉坦病毒显示出较低的序列同一性(分别为 10.9%、13.5% 和 15.1% 的分歧)。系统发育分析表明,该病毒属于正汉坦病毒安山种(ANDV),在ANDV类变种中,与Lechiguanas分支关系更为密切。在之前的研究中,类似的分歧百分比被认为足以区分类似 AND 的变体。由于该患者在发病前没有外游史,我们进行了啮齿类动物调查,以确认是否存在宿主。啮齿类动物群落与不同生态区(永加斯、查科和蒙特)之间的过渡带相适应。此外,捕获的物种之一,淡黄小鼠(Oligoryzomys flavescens),此前被描述为汉坦病毒库。正如一些作者所提出的,该物种可能在其范围内拥有多种变体,或者包含一个物种复合体。

Novel Orthohantavirus Associated with Hantavirus Pulmonary Syndrome in Northern Argentina

Authors: Carla M. Bellomo, Sebastian Kehl, Daniel Oscar Alonso, Walter López, Flavia Cassinelli, Rocío María Coelho, Gabriela Bravo, Sara Aguirre, Marcela Dib, Natalia Periolo
Original Abstract
In this work, we performed the genetic characterization of a new variant of orthohantavirus associated with a fatal case of hantavirus pulmonary syndrome, outside the known endemic region, in northwestern Argentina. We first confirmed an orthohantavirus infection by ELISA, testing for the detection of IgM and IgG antibodies. Then, we extracted RNA from 100 microliters of serum, the only sample available, followed by RT-PCR. The amplicons were sequenced using Sanger and next-generation sequencing technology. We obtained partial sequences of 1253 bp, 799 bp and 1675 bp from the S-, M- and L-segments, respectively, showing low sequence identities with all the previously characterized hantaviruses (10.9%, 13.5% and 15.1% of the divergence, respectively). The phylogenetic analysis showed that this virus belongs to the Orthohantavirus andesense species (ANDV), and among the ANDV-like variants, it is more closely related to the Lechiguanas clade. Similar percentages of divergence were considered sufficient to distinguish AND-like variants in previous works. As the patient had no travel history before the onset of disease was reported, we conducted rodent surveys to confirm the presence of reservoirs. The rodent assemblage was compatible with the transitional zone among different ecoregions (Yungas, Chaco and Monte). Moreover, one of the species captured, Oligoryzomys flavescens, was previously described as a reservoir of hantavirus. This species may either host several variants across its range or encompass a species complex, as proposed by some authors.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究 · E1

对地方性汉坦病毒肺综合征区中的正汉坦病毒储存库淡黄小鼠的相对丰度进行建模

Modelling relative abundance of Oligoryzomys flavescens , an Orthohantavirus reservoir, in an endemic hantavirus pulmonary syndrome zone
Authors: María Victoria Vadell, Carlos González Fischer, Mariano Codesido, Aníbal Carbajo, David Bilenca, Isabel E. Gómez Villafañe
摘要中文翻译摘要 汉坦病毒肺综合征(HPS)是由仓鼠科啮齿动物寄主的新世界正汉坦病毒(汉坦病毒科)引起​​的一种人畜共患新发传染病。在阿根廷,其主要宿主之一是乙齿鼠类啮齿动物淡黄鼠,这是一种广泛分布于阿根廷中东部潘帕斯草原、三角洲和埃斯皮纳尔生态区的小鼠。由于储存库的丰度及其在啮齿动物群落中的比例会影响病毒流行率和人类暴露风险,因此在其已知地理分布范围内的估计对于设计预防 HPS 的公共卫生策略至关重要。因此,本研究的目的是模拟阿根廷布宜诺斯艾利斯省大部分潘帕斯生态区中苦参的相对丰度,该地区汉坦病毒肺综合征流行。为此,我们使用了 2006 年至 2008 年间从布宜诺斯艾利斯省大部分地区的 51 个地点收集的猫头鹰颗粒样本。通过检查头骨、齿骨和臼齿,将每个颗粒中的哺乳动物猎物识别到尽可能低的分类水平。我们将每个样本中发现的苦参的频率建模为每个地点的气候、环境和地形数据的函数。两个最佳模型被应用于地理参考信息系统,以构建布宜诺斯艾利斯省内估计频率的地图(作为相对丰度的代表)。布宜诺斯艾利斯省苦参的估计相对丰度与年平均温度、年降水量和淡水体的存在显着相关,并且在次区域之间存在差异,其中内陆和滚动潘帕斯是频率最高的区域。了解哪些地区的苦参丰度预计较高,可将有限的卫生工作集中在最需要的地区,以减少传播并增加检测。

Modelling relative abundance of Oligoryzomys flavescens , an Orthohantavirus reservoir, in an endemic hantavirus pulmonary syndrome zone

Authors: María Victoria Vadell, Carlos González Fischer, Mariano Codesido, Aníbal Carbajo, David Bilenca, Isabel E. Gómez Villafañe
Original Abstract
Abstract Hantavirus pulmonary syndrome (HPS) is a zoonotic emerging infectious disease caused by New World orthohantaviruses (family Hantaviridae ) hosted by rodents of the family Cricetidae. In Argentina, one of its main hosts is the sigmodontine rodent Oligoryzomys flavescens , a widely distributed mouse of the Pampas, Delta and Espinal ecoregions of central‐east Argentina. Because the abundance of the reservoir and its proportion in the rodent community affects both virus prevalence and human exposure risk, its estimation throughout its known geographical distribution is of key importance for the design of public health strategies to prevent HPS. The aim of this study was therefore to model the relative abundance of O. flavescens in most of the Pampas ecoregion within Buenos Aires Province, Argentina, where hantavirus pulmonary syndrome is endemic. To do this we used owl‐pellet samples collected between 2006 and 2008 from 51 sites distributed throughout most of Buenos Aires province. Mammalian prey in each pellet was identified to the lowest possible taxonomic level by examination of the skulls, dentaries and molars. We modelled the frequency of O. flavescens found in each sample as a function of climatic, environmental, and topographic data of each site. The two best models were applied to a Geo referential Information System to build maps of estimated frequency (as a proxy of relative abundance) within Buenos Aires province. Estimated relative abundance of O. flavescens in Buenos Aires province was significantly associated with annual mean temperature, annual precipitation and presence of freshwater bodies, and varied among sub‐regions, with the Inland and Rolling Pampas being the regions with highest frequencies. Knowing in which areas O. flavescens abundance is expected to be higher can be used to concentrate limited sanitary efforts in those areas that are most needed in order to reduce transmission and increase detection.

全长汉滩病毒聚合酶寡聚成对称二聚体和六聚体的结构表征

Structural characterization of the oligomerization of full-length Hantaan virus polymerase into symmetric dimers and hexamers
Authors: Quentin Durieux Trouilleton, Dominique Housset, Paco Tarillon, Benoît Arragain, Hélène Malet
摘要中文翻译摘要 汉滩病毒是一种危险的人类病原体,其分段负链RNA基因组由病毒编码的多功能聚合酶复制和转录。在这里,我们描述了几种寡聚形式的汉坦病毒聚合酶的完整冷冻电镜结构。Apo 聚合酶原聚体可以采用两种截然不同的构象,它们组装成两个不同的对称同二聚体,它们本身可以聚集形成六聚体。聚合酶二聚化诱导大多数聚合酶结构域的稳定,包括对二聚体界面贡献最大的 C 端结构域,以及参与聚合酶稳定的套索区域。与病毒RNA的结合会引起显着的构象变化,导致对称寡聚体破坏和聚合酶激活,这表明apo多聚体可能作为保护系统参与其中,从而稳定原本灵活的C端结构域。总的来说,这些结果提供了对汉坦病毒聚合酶的多聚化能力的深入了解,并可能有助于定义抗病毒化合物来对抗这些危及生命的病毒。

Structural characterization of the oligomerization of full-length Hantaan virus polymerase into symmetric dimers and hexamers

Authors: Quentin Durieux Trouilleton, Dominique Housset, Paco Tarillon, Benoît Arragain, Hélène Malet
Original Abstract
Abstract Hantaan virus is a dangerous human pathogen whose segmented negative-stranded RNA genome is replicated and transcribed by a virally-encoded multi-functional polymerase. Here we describe the complete cryo-electron microscopy structure of Hantaan virus polymerase in several oligomeric forms. Apo polymerase protomers can adopt two drastically different conformations, which assemble into two distinct symmetric homodimers, that can themselves gather to form hexamers. Polymerase dimerization induces the stabilization of most polymerase domains, including the C-terminal domain that contributes the most to dimer’s interface, along with a lariat region that participates to the polymerase steadying. Binding to viral RNA induces significant conformational changes resulting in symmetric oligomer disruption and polymerase activation, suggesting the possible involvement of apo multimers as protecting systems that would stabilize the otherwise flexible C-terminal domains. Overall, these results provide insights into the multimerization capability of Hantavirus polymerase and may help to define antiviral compounds to counteract these life-threatening viruses.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究 · E1

巴西自然宿主种群中汉坦病毒的扩张趋势

Hantavirus Expansion Trends in Natural Host Populations in Brazil
Authors: José Henrique Fortes Mello, Renata L. Muylaert, Carlos Eduardo Viveiros Grelle
摘要中文翻译汉坦病毒是导致美洲汉坦病毒心肺综合征(HCPS)的人畜共患病原体,巴西在南美洲确诊的 HCPS 病例数中排名第一。在这项研究中,我们通过将 Kermack-McCormick SIR 模型与细胞自动机模型 (CA) 结合来模拟高致命性汉坦病毒在自然宿主中的每月传播,因此使用最近编制的主要宿主物种丰度和已确认的汉坦病毒感染死亡数据,同时评估宿主群体中的细胞内和细胞间感染动态。对于这两种宿主物种,我们的模型预测感染面积将会增加,迄今为止尚未确诊病例的 22 个城市预计将在未来十年内至少出现一例,并且 11 个城市的感染人数将减少。我们的研究结果支持了现有的研究,并揭示了汉坦病毒可能在公认的震中传播的新区域。我们强调时空趋势和潜在扩张,强调普遍的栖息地破碎化和农业扩张带来的风险增加。持续的预防工作和“同一个健康”行动至关重要,特别是在新确定的高风险城市。

Hantavirus Expansion Trends in Natural Host Populations in Brazil

Authors: José Henrique Fortes Mello, Renata L. Muylaert, Carlos Eduardo Viveiros Grelle
Original Abstract
Hantaviruses are zoonotic agents responsible for causing Hantavirus Cardiopulmonary Syndrome (HCPS) in the Americas, with Brazil ranking first in number of confirmed HCPS cases in South America. In this study, we simulate the monthly spread of highly lethal hantavirus in natural hosts by conjugating a Kermack–McCormick SIR model with a cellular automata model (CA), therefore simultaneously evaluating both in-cell and between-cell infection dynamics in host populations, using recently compiled data on main host species abundances and confirmed deaths by hantavirus infection. For both host species, our models predict an increase in the area of infection, with 22 municipalities where no cases have been confirmed to date expected to have at least one case in the next decade, and a reduction in infection in 11 municipalities. Our findings support existing research and reveal new areas where hantavirus is likely to spread within recognized epicenters. Highlighting spatial-temporal trends and potential expansion, we emphasize the increased risk due to pervasive habitat fragmentation and agricultural expansion. Consistent prevention efforts and One Health actions are crucial, especially in newly identified high-risk municipalities.

巴西南部不同地理区域野生啮齿动物汉坦病毒感染流行率和人类抗汉坦病毒血清学特征

Hantavirus Infection Prevalence in Wild Rodents and Human Anti-Hantavirus Serological Profiles from Different Geographic Areas of South Brazil
Authors: Sonia M. Raboni, Adriana Delfraro, Luana de Borba, Bernardo R. Teixeira, Vanessa Stella, Marina R. de Araujo, Suzana Carstensen, Giselia Rubio, Angela Maron, Elba R. S. Lemos
摘要中文翻译巴拉那州是巴西汉坦病毒肺综合征累计病例数第四高的州。为了绘制汉坦病毒传播的风险区域,我们进行了一项基于啮齿动物诱捕的研究,并确定了这些动物和这些地区居民中的抗汉坦病毒血清流行率。啮齿动物和人类的总体血清阳性率分别为 2.5% 和 2.4%。对百分之八十二的血清反应呈阳性的啮齿动物进行了基因分析。系统发育分析显示,来自啮齿动物样本的汉坦病毒与 Araucária(Juquitiba 样)或 Jaborá 汉坦病毒基因型聚类。Jaborá 菌株在 Akodon serrensis 和 Akodon montensis 中被鉴定,而 Araucária 菌株在 Oligoryzomys nigripes、Oxymycterus judex、A. montensis 和 Akodon paranaensis 中被检测到,后者是首次被鉴定为天然宿主。这些发现揭示了巴西病毒与宿主之间的复杂关系,这可能对汉坦病毒在自然和人类流行病学中的传播动态产生影响。

Hantavirus Infection Prevalence in Wild Rodents and Human Anti-Hantavirus Serological Profiles from Different Geographic Areas of South Brazil

Authors: Sonia M. Raboni, Adriana Delfraro, Luana de Borba, Bernardo R. Teixeira, Vanessa Stella, Marina R. de Araujo, Suzana Carstensen, Giselia Rubio, Angela Maron, Elba R. S. Lemos
Original Abstract
Paraná state presents the fourth highest number of accumulated cases of hantavirus pulmonary syndrome in Brazil. To map the risk areas for hantavirus transmission we carried out a study based on rodent trapping and determined the anti-hantavirus seroprevalence in these animals and in the inhabitants of these localities. Overall seroprevalence in rodents and humans were 2.5% and 2.4%, respectively. Eighty-two percent of the seropositive rodents were genetically analyzed. Phylogenetic analyses revealed that hantaviruses from rodent samples cluster with Araucária (Juquitiba-like) or Jaborá hantavirus genotypes. The Jaborá strain was identified in Akodon serrensis and Akodon montensis , whereas the Araucária strain was detected in Oligoryzomys nigripes , Oxymycterus judex , A. montensis , and Akodon paranaensis , with the latter species being identified for the first time as a natural host. These findings expose the complex relationships between virus and reservoirs in Brazil, which could have an impact on hantavirus transmission dynamics in nature and human epidemiology.

问题与背景
巴拉那州是巴西汉坦病毒肺综合征累计病例数第四高的州。为了绘制汉坦病毒传播的风险区域,我们进行了一项基于啮齿动物诱捕的研究,并确定了这些动物和这些地区居民中的抗汉坦病毒血清流行率。啮齿动物和人类的总体血清阳性率分别为 2.5% 和 2.4%。

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究 · E2

与细胞毒性 T 淋巴细胞相关抗原 4 胞外域融合可增强汉滩病毒 DNA 疫苗在 C57BL/6 小鼠中的免疫原性

Fusion with extracellular domain of cytotoxic T-lymphocyte-associated-antigen 4 leads to enhancement of immunogenicity of Hantaan virus DNA vaccines in C57BL/6 mice
Authors: Feng Liu, Mifang Liang, Shouchun Cao, Qinzhi Liu, Quanfu Zhang, Chuan Li, Shuo Zhang, Shiwen Wang, Dexin Li
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景汉滩病毒(HTNV) 是最严重的啮齿动物传播疾病肾综合征出血热 (HFRS) 的病原体。需要一种安全有效的 HTNV 疫苗。在动物模型中,用表达融合抗原和生物活性因子的 DNA 构建体进行疫苗接种已显示出有望改善病毒制剂的免疫原性,但融合策略对 HTNV DNA 疫苗的影响尚未得到研究。结果 构建了编码 HTNV 核衣壳蛋白 (N) 和糖蛋白(Gn 和 Gc)的 DNA 质粒,与靶向抗原呈递细胞 (APC) 的细胞毒性 T 淋巴细胞相关抗原 4 (eCTLA-4) 的胞外结构域融合。用表达 eCTLA-4-HTNV-N/GP 融合蛋白的质粒对小鼠进行肌内免疫,可显着增强 C57BL/6 小鼠的特异性抗体反应以及细胞毒性 T 淋巴细胞 (CTL) 反应。此外,当与 CpG 基序共同施用时,这种效果可能会进一步增强。结论 病毒抗原与生物活性因子融合的修饰有望赋予小鼠有效的抗原呈递并提高 DNA 疫苗的效力。

Fusion with extracellular domain of cytotoxic T-lymphocyte-associated-antigen 4 leads to enhancement of immunogenicity of Hantaan virus DNA vaccines in C57BL/6 mice

Authors: Feng Liu, Mifang Liang, Shouchun Cao, Qinzhi Liu, Quanfu Zhang, Chuan Li, Shuo Zhang, Shiwen Wang, Dexin Li
Original Abstract
Abstract Background Hantaan virus (HTNV) is the causative agent of the most severe form of a rodent-borne disease known as hemorrhagic fever with renal syndrome (HFRS). A safe and effective HTNV vaccine is needed. Vaccination with DNA constructs expressing fused antigen with bioactive factors, has shown promising improvement of immunogenicity for viral agents in animal models, but the effect of fusion strategy on HTNV DNA vaccine has not been investigated. Results DNA plasmids encoding the HTNV nucleocapsid protein (N) and glycoprotein (Gn and Gc) in fusion to the extracellular domain of cytotoxic T-lymphocyte-associated-antigen 4 (eCTLA-4) targeting to antigen presenting cells (APCs) were constructed. Intramuscular immunization of mice with plasmids expressing eCTLA-4-HTNV-N/GP fusion proteins leads to a significant enhancement of the specific antibody response as well as cytotoxic T-lymphocyte (CTL) response in C57BL/6 mice. Moreover, this effect could be further augmented when co-administered with CpG motifs. Conclusions Modification of viral antigen in fusion to bioactive factor will be promising to confer efficient antigen presentation and improve the potency of DNA vaccine in mice.
