Journal: PLOS Neglected Tropical Diseases  |  Online: 2023-07-24  |  Database created: 2023-07-24  |  Report date: 2026-07-15  |  DOI: 10.1371/journal.pntd.0010806  |  Citation: 17, 7, e0010806
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究 · E2

基于污染与气象因素联合滞后效应的肾综合征出血热时间序列分析及预测模型构建

Time series analyses based on the joint lagged effect analysis of pollution and meteorological factors of hemorrhagic fever with renal syndrome and the construction of prediction model
Authors: Ye Chen, Weiming Hou, Jing Dong
摘要中文翻译背景 肾综合征出血热(HFRS)是由汉坦病毒引起的一种与啮齿动物相关的人畜共患疾病。以往的研究已经明确了气象因素对HFRS发生的影响,但很少有研究关注污染和气象因素对HFRS的滞后效应和相互作用的分层分析。方法 收集沉阳市2005-2019年HFRS病例的气象、污染物和流行病学数据。采用季节性自回归积分移动平均(SARIMA)模型来预测 HFRS 的发生率,并与 Holt-Winters 三参数指数平滑模型进行比较。采用最大滞后期为16天的分布式滞后非线性模型(DLNM)来评估污染和气象因素对HFRS病例的滞后性、分层和极端影响,然后采用广义加性模型(GAM)来探讨SO 2 和其他两个气象因素对HFRS病例的相互作用。结果SARIMA月度模型比自己的季度模型和Holt-Winters模型具有更好的拟合度和预测能力,最优模型为(1,1,0) (2,1,0) 12。总体而言,包括湿度、风速和SO 2 在内的环境因素与HFRS的发生相关,并且存在非线性的暴露-滞后-反应关联。极高的SO 2 增加了HFRS发生的风险,最大RR值:2.583(95%CI:1.145,5.827)。极低风和低SO 2 对HFRS感染具有显着的保护作用,最小RR值分别为0.487(95%CI:0.260,0.912)和0.577(95%CI:0.370,0.898)。交互作用表明,当每日SO 2 增加和湿度降低时,HFRS 感染的风险达到最高。结论 SARIMA 模型可能有助于增强基于长期数据集的每月 HFRS 发病率的预测。我们的研究表明,湿度和SO 2 等环境因素对HFRS的发生有延迟影响,并且湿度的影响会受到SO 2 和风速的影响。公共卫生专业人员应在低湿度、低风条件下以及 SO 2 水平高于 200 μg/m 3 后 2-3 天更加小心地控制 HFRS。

Time series analyses based on the joint lagged effect analysis of pollution and meteorological factors of hemorrhagic fever with renal syndrome and the construction of prediction model

Authors: Ye Chen, Weiming Hou, Jing Dong
Original Abstract
Background Hemorrhagic fever with renal syndrome (HFRS) is a rodent-related zoonotic disease induced by hantavirus. Previous studies have identified the influence of meteorological factors on the onset of HFRS, but few studies have focused on the stratified analysis of the lagged effects and interactions of pollution and meteorological factors on HFRS. Methods We collected meteorological, contaminant and epidemiological data on cases of HFRS in Shenyang from 2005–2019. A seasonal autoregressive integrated moving average (SARIMA) model was used to predict the incidence of HFRS and compared with Holt-Winters three-parameter exponential smoothing model. A distributed lag nonlinear model (DLNM) with a maximum lag period of 16 days was applied to assess the lag, stratification and extreme effects of pollution and meteorological factors on HFRS cases, followed by a generalized additive model (GAM) to explore the interaction of SO 2 and two other meteorological factors on HFRS cases. Results The SARIMA monthly model has better fit and forecasting power than its own quarterly model and the Holt-Winters model, with an optimal model of (1,1,0) (2,1,0) 12 . Overall, environmental factors including humidity, wind speed and SO 2 were correlated with the onset of HFRS and there was a non-linear exposure-lag-response association. Extremely high SO 2 increased the risk of HFRS incidence, with the maximum RR values: 2.583 (95%CI:1.145,5.827). Extremely low windy and low SO 2 played a significant protective role on HFRS infection, with the minimum RR values: 0.487 (95%CI:0.260,0.912) and 0.577 (95%CI:0.370,0.898), respectively. Interaction indicated that the risk of HFRS infection reached its highest when increasing daily SO 2 and decreasing humidity. Conclusions The SARIMA model may help to enhance the forecast of monthly HFRS incidence based on a long-range dataset. Our study had shown that environmental factors such as humidity and SO 2 have a delayed effect on the occurrence of HFRS and that the effect of humidity can be influenced by SO 2 and wind speed. Public health professionals should take greater care in controlling HFRS in low humidity, low windy conditions and 2–3 days after SO 2 levels above 200 μg/m 3 .

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究 · E1

俄罗斯鞑靼斯坦致命性肾综合征出血热病例中的细胞因子风暴与体液免疫反应缺陷相结合

Cytokine Storm Combined with Humoral Immune Response Defect in Fatal Hemorrhagic Fever with Renal Syndrome Case, Tatarstan, Russia
Authors: Ekaterina Garanina, Ekaterina Martynova, Yuriy Davidyuk, Emmanuel Kabwe, Konstantin Ivanov, Angelina Titova, Maria Markelova, Margarita Zhuravleva, Georgiy Cherepnev, Venera G. Shakirova
摘要中文翻译肾综合征出血热 (HFRS) 在鞑靼斯坦共和国流行,每年登记数千例病例。普马拉正汉坦病毒通常在人类病例样本以及捕获的啮齿动物宿主银行田鼠中检测到。尽管细胞因子风暴假说已被广泛接受,但 HFRS 的发病机制仍未得到很好的描述。在这项研究中,我们对 1 例致命 HFRS 病例与 24 例非致命病例进行了全面分析,其中使用免疫学、组织学、遗传和临床方法检测了体液和细胞免疫反应的激活、促炎细胞因子和凝血紊乱。多器官功能衰竭合并弥散性血管内凝血综合征和急性肾功能衰竭是死亡原因。发现白细胞介素 (IL)-7 血清水平降低,IL-18、趋化因子(C-C 基序)配体 (CCL)-5、干细胞生长因子 (SCGF)-b 和肿瘤坏死因子-β (TNF-β) 血清水平升高,支持汉坦病毒发病机制的细胞因子风暴假说。

Cytokine Storm Combined with Humoral Immune Response Defect in Fatal Hemorrhagic Fever with Renal Syndrome Case, Tatarstan, Russia

Authors: Ekaterina Garanina, Ekaterina Martynova, Yuriy Davidyuk, Emmanuel Kabwe, Konstantin Ivanov, Angelina Titova, Maria Markelova, Margarita Zhuravleva, Georgiy Cherepnev, Venera G. Shakirova
Original Abstract
Hemorrhagic fever with renal syndrome (HFRS) is endemic in Tatarstan, where thousands of cases are registered annually. Puumala orthohantavirus is commonly detected in human case samples as well as in captured bank voles, the rodent hosts. The pathogenesis of HFRS is still not well described, although the cytokine storm hypothesis is largely accepted. In this study, we present a comprehensive analysis of a fatal HFRS case compared with twenty four non-fatal cases where activation of the humoral and cellular immune responses, pro-inflammatory cytokines and disturbed blood coagulation were detected using immunological, histological, genetic and clinical approaches. Multiple organ failure combined with disseminated intravascular coagulation syndrome and acute renal failure was the cause of death. Decreased Interleukin (IL)-7 and increased IL-18, chemokine (C-C motif) ligand (CCL)-5, stem cell growth factor (SCGF)-b and tumor necrosis factor-beta (TNF-β) serum levels were found, supporting the cytokine storm hypothesis of hantavirus pathogenesis.

Journal: Baylor University Medical Center Proceedings  |  Online: 2026-07-16  |  Print: 2026-07-16  |  Database created: 2026-07-16  |  Report date: 2026-07-16  |  DOI: 10.1080/08998280.2026.2698359  |  Citation: 2026, 1-10
A 高优先级学术文献 · 综述 · E1

儿童汉坦病毒心肺综合征:对表现、重症监护管理和结果的系统范围审查

Hantavirus cardiopulmonary syndrome in children: a systematic scoping review of presentation, critical care management, and outcomes
Authors: Weerapong Lilitwat, Neha Mahindrakar, Prakreeti Bhandari, Ramya Yedatore, Shristi Maharjan, Maryam Zubair, Yuttiwat Vorakunthada, Lilitwat W, Mahindrakar N, Bhandari P
摘要中文翻译背景:儿童汉坦病毒心肺综合征/汉坦病毒肺综合征(HCPS/HPS)很少见,但可以从非特异性发热前驱症状迅速进展为呼吸衰竭、休克和多器官功能障碍。儿科特异性证据有限,并且缺乏全面的临床文献图谱。方法:我们根据系统审查和范围审查元分析的首选报告项目 (PRISMA-ScR) 指南(在 DOI:10.17605/OSF.IO/MZDN4 注册的协议)进行了系统范围审查。PubMed/MEDLINE、Embase、Scopus、Web of Science 和 Cochrane CENTRAL 的检索时间从最初到 2026 年 5 月。符合条件的报告描述了具有 HCPS/HPS 的儿童或青少年(年龄 ≤ 18 岁)和可提取的儿科临床数据。两名评审员使用乔安娜·布里格斯研究所的工具独立筛选记录、评估重叠、提取数据并评估报告质量。结果:在 2208 条数据库记录中,经过删除 976 份重复项和筛选后,20 份报告被纳入核心儿科综合报告中。报告集中在美洲。复发特征包括发烧、胃肠道症状、肌痛、血小板减少、血液浓缩、肺水肿、呼吸衰竭和休克。严重病例需要机械通气、血管活性支持、体外膜氧合,在某些情况下还需要肾脏替代治疗。更大的儿科病例系列和监测水平报告报告死亡率约为 33% 至 37%。证据主要是基于案例的,报告的完整性各不相同。结论:儿童 HCPS/HPS 很少见,但可能会迅速致命。在流行地区或啮齿类动物暴露后及早怀疑,及时提供支持性重症监护并及时考虑高级心肺支持,是管理的核心。

Hantavirus cardiopulmonary syndrome in children: a systematic scoping review of presentation, critical care management, and outcomes

Authors: Weerapong Lilitwat, Neha Mahindrakar, Prakreeti Bhandari, Ramya Yedatore, Shristi Maharjan, Maryam Zubair, Yuttiwat Vorakunthada, Lilitwat W, Mahindrakar N, Bhandari P
Original Abstract
BACKGROUND: Pediatric hantavirus cardiopulmonary syndrome/hantavirus pulmonary syndrome (HCPS/HPS) is rare but can progress rapidly from a nonspecific febrile prodrome to respiratory failure, shock, and multiorgan dysfunction. Pediatric-specific evidence is limited, and a comprehensive map of the clinical literature is lacking. METHODS: We conducted a systematic scoping review per Preferred Reporting Items for Systematic Reviews and Meta-Analyses for Scoping Reviews (PRISMA-ScR) guidance (protocol registered at DOI:10.17605/OSF.IO/MZDN4). PubMed/MEDLINE, Embase, Scopus, Web of Science, and Cochrane CENTRAL were searched from inception to May 2026. Eligible reports described children or adolescents (age ≤18 years) with HCPS/HPS and extractable pediatric clinical data. Two reviewers independently screened records, assessed overlap, extracted data, and appraised reporting quality using Joanna Briggs Institute tools. RESULTS: Of 2208 database records, 20 reports were included in the core pediatric synthesis after removal of 976 duplicates and screening. Reports were concentrated in the Americas. Recurrent features included fever, gastrointestinal symptoms, myalgia, thrombocytopenia, hemoconcentration, pulmonary edema, respiratory failure, and shock. Severe cases required mechanical ventilation, vasoactive support, extracorporeal membrane oxygenation, and in some cases renal replacement therapy. Larger pediatric case series and surveillance-level reports reported mortality of approximately 33% to 37%. Evidence was predominantly case-based with variable reporting completeness. CONCLUSIONS: Pediatric HCPS/HPS is rare but potentially rapidly fatal. Early suspicion in endemic regions or after rodent exposure, with prompt supportive critical care and timely consideration of advanced cardiopulmonary support, is central to management.
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
儿童汉坦病毒心肺综合征/汉坦病毒肺综合征(HCPS/HPS)很少见,但可以从非特异性发热前驱症状迅速进展为呼吸衰竭、休克和多器官功能障碍。儿科特异性证据有限,并且缺乏全面的临床文献图谱。
证据基础与方法
我们根据范围界定审查的系统审查和元分析的首选报告项目 (PRISMA-ScR) 指南(在 DOI:10.17605/OSF.IO/MZDN4 注册的协议)进行了系统范围界定审查。PubMed/MEDLINE、Embase、Scopus、Web of Science 和 Cochrane CENTRAL 的检索时间从最初到 2026 年 5 月。符合条件的报告描述了具有 HCPS/HPS 的儿童或青少年(年龄 ≤ 18 岁)和可提取的儿科临床数据。
共识与结论
在 2208 条数据库记录中,经过删除 976 条重复项和筛选后,有 20 条报告被纳入儿科核心综合报告中。报告集中在美洲。复发特征包括发烧、胃肠道症状、肌痛、血小板减少、血液浓缩、肺水肿、呼吸衰竭和休克。
争议与缺口
所提供的证据中没有明确报告争议和证据差距。
科研与实践启示
更大的儿科病例系列和监测水平报告报告死亡率约为 33% 至 37%。

A 高优先级学术文献 · 综述 · E1

阿拉伯联合酋长国护理本科生的汉坦病毒防范情况:知识、风险认知和预防实践的横断面研究

Hantavirus preparedness among undergraduate nursing students in the United Arab Emirates: a cross-sectional study of knowledge, risk perception and preventive practice
Authors: Fatma M. Ibrahim, Ghada Shahrour, Ibrahim Al Faouri, Israa Abuijlan, Amal Mohammed Al Balushi, Shaimaa Abd-Elsamad, Eman Abdelaziz Dabou, Ibrahim FM, Shahrour G, Al Faouri I
摘要中文翻译背景:汉坦病毒感染在许多情况下并不常见,但可导致严重的肺部或肾脏疾病。护理专业的学生需要在社区和临床学习期间针对具体疾病做好暴露史记录、安全预防咨询和感染预防的准备。本研究调查了本科护生对汉坦病毒的知识、风险认知和预防实践,并确定了相关因素。方法:2025-2026 学年,对阿拉伯联合酋长国哈伊马角一个护理项目的 236 名护理本科生进行了一项描述性分析横断面调查。使用研究人员开发的护理学生汉坦病毒知识、态度和预防实践问卷(HKAPP-NS)收集数据。使用描述性统计、Cronbach's alpha、Pearson 相关性和具有 HC3 稳健标准误的多变量线性回归。结果:236 名完整案例受访者的知识水平较低(平均 8.13/20,标准差 5.84),其中 59.0% 的人知识水平较低。态度中等(平均 33.34/50,SD 7.78),总体实践较高(平均 42.98/64,SD 15.83)。跨领域的内部一致性很强(alpha 0.886-0.967)。态度与总体实践相关(r = 0.569,p < 0.001),而知识则不然(r = 0.101,p = 0.120)。以前的汉坦病毒教学、感染防控培训和环境暴露预示着更高的知识。态度是整体实践的最强独立预测因素。结论:学生报告了相对频繁的预防措施,但在特定疾病方面存在重大知识差距,特别是在安全啮齿动物污染清理、常规疫苗可用性、特定抗病毒治疗和人际传播方面。研究结果表明,态度而非知识独立预测了实践,这表明基于情景的“One Health”教育应将事实教学与风险评估、自我效能建设、接触史记录和预防咨询结合起来,以加强未来护士对啮齿动物传播的人畜共患感染的准备。

Hantavirus preparedness among undergraduate nursing students in the United Arab Emirates: a cross-sectional study of knowledge, risk perception and preventive practice

Authors: Fatma M. Ibrahim, Ghada Shahrour, Ibrahim Al Faouri, Israa Abuijlan, Amal Mohammed Al Balushi, Shaimaa Abd-Elsamad, Eman Abdelaziz Dabou, Ibrahim FM, Shahrour G, Al Faouri I
Original Abstract
BACKGROUND: Hantavirus infection is uncommon in many settings but can cause severe pulmonary or renal disease. Nursing students require disease-specific preparedness for exposure-history taking, safe prevention counselling and infection prevention during community and clinical learning. This study examined undergraduate nursing students' knowledge, risk perception and preventive practice toward hantavirus and identified associated factors. METHODS: A descriptive analytical cross-sectional survey was conducted among 236 undergraduate nursing students in one nursing program in Ras Al Khaimah, the United Arab Emirates during the 2025-2026 academic year. Data was collected using the researcher-developed Hantavirus Knowledge, Attitudes and Preventive Practices Questionnaire for Nursing Students (HKAPP-NS). Descriptive statistics, Cronbach's alpha, Pearson correlations and multivariable linear regression with HC3 robust standard errors were used. RESULTS: Knowledge was low among the 236 complete-case respondents (mean 8.13/20, SD 5.84), with 59.0% classified as having poor knowledge. Attitude was moderate (mean 33.34/50, SD 7.78), and overall practice was higher (mean 42.98/64, SD 15.83). Internal consistency was strong across domains (alpha 0.886-0.967). Attitude correlated with overall practice (r = 0.569, p < 0.001), whereas knowledge did not (r = 0.101, p = 0.120). Previous hantavirus teaching, infection prevention and control training and environmental exposure predicted higher knowledge. Attitude was the strongest independent predictor of overall practice. CONCLUSIONS: Students reported comparatively frequent precautionary practice but had important disease-specific knowledge gaps, particularly regarding safe rodent-contamination cleanup, routine vaccine availability, specific antiviral treatment and person-to-person transmission. The finding that attitude, rather than knowledge, independently predicted practice suggests that scenario-based One Health education should combine factual teaching with risk appraisal, self-efficacy building, exposure-history taking and prevention counselling to strengthen future nurses' preparedness for rodent-borne zoonotic infections.

安第斯山脉病毒——临床回顾

Andes Virus — A Clinical Review
Authors: Gavin H. Harris, Anabel Sinchi, James Lawler, Maria Frank, Gavin H Harris
摘要中文翻译安第斯病毒(ANDV)是唯一有记录的人际传播的正汉坦病毒。我们根据已发表的专家共识指南、现场经验和临床试验,总结了 ANDV 感染的流行病学和临床特征,并回顾了临床管理的最佳实践。我们还评估当前可用的和正在研究的治疗方法(包括抗病毒药物的使用),评估新兴的单克隆抗体疗法,并概述疫苗开发的前景。最后,我们讨论重要的感染预防和控制措施。

Andes Virus — A Clinical Review

Authors: Gavin H. Harris, Anabel Sinchi, James Lawler, Maria Frank, Gavin H Harris
Original Abstract
Andes virus (ANDV) is the sole orthohantavirus with documented human-to-human transmission. We summarize the epidemiology and clinical features of ANDV infection and review best practices in clinical management, as based on published expert consensus guidelines, field experience, and clinical trials. We also evaluate currently available and investigational treatments (including the use of antiviral agents), assess emerging monoclonal antibody therapies, and outline prospects for vaccine development. Finally, we discuss important infection prevention and control measures.

肾综合征出血热出血:汉坦病毒感染止血丧失特征的系统评价

Bleeding in haemorrhagic fever with renal syndrome: A systematic review characterising the loss of haemostasis in hantavirus infections
Authors: Matthew J. Riley, Aliza Hudda, Miša Korva, Tatjana Avšič-Županc, Beverley J. Hunt, Tom E. Fletcher, Riley MJ, Hudda A, Korva M, Avšič-Županc T
摘要中文翻译背景:肾综合征出血热(HFRS)由汉坦病毒引起,与不同程度的血小板减少、凝血异常和出血表现相关。虽然多项研究已经描述了临床特征,但之前没有系统评价综合 HFRS 止血功能障碍的证据。本综述旨在描述 HFRS 凝血障碍的临床和实验室特征,为了解疾病机制和未来研究提供信息。方法/主要发现:我们于 2024 年 12 月 12 日系统地检索了 MEDLINE、PubMed、CINAHL、Web of Science 和 Scopus,以查找报告确诊 HFRS 中止血功能障碍的临床或实验室证据的研究。该审查方案已在 PROSPERO 上前瞻性注册(CRD42024618760)。符合预定纳入标准的观察性研究和病例系列是合格的。使用乔安娜·布里格斯研究所 (JBI) 关键评估工具和美国国立卫生研究院 (NIH) 质量评估工具(对于一项前后队列研究)评估偏倚风险。根据方法学质量,研究从 1(最低)到 3(最高)分级。由于研究设计和结果报告的异质性,荟萃分析不可行。计算实验室参数的加权平均值,并使用气泡图和条形图可视化数据。纳入了 55 项研究,涉及 7,950 名患者。血小板减少症是最一致的异常,通常在汉坦 (HTNV)、多布拉瓦 (DOBV) 和首尔 (SEOV) 病毒感染中比普马拉 (PUUV) 病毒感染更为明显,后者与较轻的疾病相关。据报道,活化部分凝血活酶时间轻度延长,D-二聚体升高。有限的儿科数据表明疾病较轻,凝血异常不太明显。结论/意义:本综述首次系统总结了 HFRS 的止血功能障碍。研究结果强调,与 PUUV 相比,HTNV、DOBV 和 SEOV 感染的血小板减少和出血更为严重。研究质量参差不齐和报告不完整限制了确切的结论,强调了对凝血和出血结果进行标准化、前瞻性研究的必要性。

Bleeding in haemorrhagic fever with renal syndrome: A systematic review characterising the loss of haemostasis in hantavirus infections

Authors: Matthew J. Riley, Aliza Hudda, Miša Korva, Tatjana Avšič-Županc, Beverley J. Hunt, Tom E. Fletcher, Riley MJ, Hudda A, Korva M, Avšič-Županc T
Original Abstract
BACKGROUND: Haemorrhagic fever with renal syndrome (HFRS) is caused by hantaviruses and is associated with variable thrombocytopenia, coagulation abnormalities, and bleeding manifestations. While clinical features have been described in multiple studies, no previous systematic review has synthesised evidence on haemostatic dysfunction in HFRS. This review aimed to characterise the clinical and laboratory features of coagulation disturbances in HFRS to inform understanding of disease mechanisms and future research. METHODOLOGY/PRINCIPAL FINDINGS: We systematically searched MEDLINE, PubMed, CINAHL, Web of Science, and Scopus on 12 December 2024 for studies reporting clinical or laboratory evidence of haemostatic dysfunction in confirmed HFRS. The review protocol was prospectively registered on PROSPERO (CRD42024618760). Observational studies and case series meeting predefined inclusion criteria were eligible. Risk of bias was assessed using the Joanna Briggs Institute (JBI) critical appraisal tools and, for one before-after cohort study, the National Institutes of Health (NIH) quality assessment tool. Studies were graded from 1 (lowest) to 3 (highest) based on methodological quality. Due to heterogeneity in study design and outcome reporting, meta-analysis was not feasible. Weighted averages were calculated for laboratory parameters, and data were visualised using bubble and bar plots. Fifty-five studies comprising 7,950 patients were included. Thrombocytopenia was the most consistent abnormality, typically more pronounced in infections with Hantaan (HTNV), Dobrava (DOBV), and Seoul (SEOV) viruses compared with Puumala (PUUV), which was associated with milder disease. Mild prolongation of activated partial thromboplastin time and elevated D-dimer were variably reported. Limited paediatric data suggested milder disease and less pronounced coagulation abnormalities. CONCLUSIONS/SIGNIFICANCE: This review provides the first systematic synthesis of haemostatic dysfunction in HFRS. Findings highlight more severe thrombocytopenia and bleeding in HTNV, DOBV, and SEOV infections compared with PUUV. Variable study quality and incomplete reporting limit firm conclusions, underscoring the need for standardised, prospective studies of coagulation and bleeding outcomes.

利巴韦林对安第斯病毒暴露的暴露后预防:一种观点。

Ribavirin post-exposure prophylaxis for Andes virus exposure: a viewpoint.
Authors: Brüggemann RJ, Vollaard A, de Stoppelaar S, van Hasselt JGC, Burger DM, Engel JJ, van Kampen JJA, McCall M, Smits L, Jolink H
摘要中文翻译安第斯病毒(ANDV)在汉坦病毒中是独一无二的,因为它可以在人与人之间传播。这引发了关于暴露后预防(PEP)的相关性的问题,特别是在高风险家庭、医疗保健相关或实验室暴露之后,考虑到其高病死率。利巴韦林已在体外和动物模型中表现出针对汉坦病毒的抗病毒活性,尽管支持其用于 ANDV 的临床证据仍然极其有限。这一观点总结了目前关于利巴韦林作为潜在 ANDV 暴露后 PEP 的现有证据。我们讨论了可能限制利巴韦林 PEP 适用性的知识差距,以便就在 PEP 中使用利巴韦林做出明智的决定。通过建模和模拟可视化潜在的剂量策略,并讨论潜在的剂量和持续时间。尽管利巴韦林 PEP 的生物学原理似乎令人信服,但缺乏受控的人体研究和潜在的毒性目前限制了其在高度选择性的暴露场景和暴露后不久使用的作用。

Ribavirin post-exposure prophylaxis for Andes virus exposure: a viewpoint.

Authors: Brüggemann RJ, Vollaard A, de Stoppelaar S, van Hasselt JGC, Burger DM, Engel JJ, van Kampen JJA, McCall M, Smits L, Jolink H
Original Abstract
Andes virus (ANDV) is unique among hantaviruses because person-to-person transmission is possible. This raises questions on the relevance of post-exposure prophylaxis (PEP), particularly following high-risk household, healthcare-associated, or laboratory exposures, considering its high case fatality rate. Ribavirin has demonstrated antiviral activity against hantaviruses in vitro and in animal models, although clinical evidence supporting its use for ANDV remains extremely limited. This viewpoint summarises the currently available evidence regarding ribavirin as PEP after potential ANDV exposure. We discuss the knowledge gaps that may limit the applicability of ribavirin PEP, to make informed decisions on the use of ribavirin in the setting of PEP. Potential dosing strategies are visualised by modelling and simulation, and potential dose and duration are discussed. Although the biological rationale for ribavirin PEP appears compelling, absence of controlled human studies and potential toxicity currently limit its role to highly selected exposure scenarios and use shortly after exposure.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 综述 · E2

汉坦病毒会引起大流行吗?

Can hantaviruses cause pandemics?
Authors: Haitham Abdel Basset Hashim, Ahmed Saad, Amr Saad, Haitham A Hashim
摘要中文翻译摘要 2026 年 MV Hondius 爆发的安第斯病毒 (ANDV) 再次引发了人们对汉坦病毒大流行潜力的担忧,特别是因为 ANDV 是唯一已证明可在人与人之间传播的汉坦病毒。最近的 COVID-19 大流行带来的深远负面影响证明了这些担忧和恐惧是合理的。汉坦病毒是分段负义 RNA 病毒,主要存在于啮齿类动物中,主要通过吸入受污染的气溶胶传播给人类。肾综合征出血热在欧洲和亚洲占主导地位,而汉坦病毒心肺综合征则发生在北美和南美,死亡率很高。尽管大多数汉坦病毒不具有人际传播性,但 ANDV 的爆发表明,在密切接触条件下,人与人之间的传播是可能的。与以往的大流行和世界卫生组织优先病原体的比较突显了汉坦病毒所共有的几个与大流行相关的特征,包括人畜共患起源、RNA基因组变异和环境影响。在这篇综述中,我们将试图找到“汉坦病毒能否引起下一次大流行?”这个问题的答案。

Can hantaviruses cause pandemics?

Authors: Haitham Abdel Basset Hashim, Ahmed Saad, Amr Saad, Haitham A Hashim
Original Abstract
Abstract The 2026 MV Hondius outbreak of Andes virus (ANDV) renewed concerns regarding the pandemic potential of hantaviruses, particularly because ANDV is the only hantavirus with proved human-to-human transmission. These concerns and fears are justified by the profound negative impact of the recent COVID-19 pandemic. Hantaviruses are segmented negative-sense RNA viruses maintained mainly in rodents and transmitted to man primarily through inhalation of contaminated aerosols. Hemorrhagic fever with renal syndrome predominates in Europe and Asia, whereas hantavirus cardiopulmonary syndrome occurs in North and South Americas and carries high mortality. Although most hantaviruses have no human-to-human transmissibility, ANDV outbreaks demonstrate that transmission between humans is possible under close-contact conditions. Comparison with previous pandemics and WHO priority pathogens highlights several pandemic-associated features shared by hantaviruses, including zoonotic origin, RNA genome variability, and environmental influences. In this review, we will try to find the answer for the question “Can hantaviruses cause the next pandemic?”.

汉坦病毒生态学、流行病学和疾病的全球视角

A Global Perspective on Hantavirus Ecology, Epidemiology, and Disease
Authors: Colleen B. Jonsson, Luiz Tadeu Moraes Figueiredo, Olli Vapalahti
摘要中文翻译摘要 汉坦病毒是一种地方性动物病毒,可在啮齿动物宿主中持续感染,但没有明显的疾病症状。这些病毒传播到人类可能导致两种严重疾病之一:汉坦病毒肺综合征和肾综合征出血热。近年来,随着美洲疫情数量的增加,人们对这些病毒的流行病学、发病机制和自然史有了更深入的了解。在这篇综述中,提出了有关这些严重人类病原体的生态学和相关疾病的当前概念。未来研究的重点表明,通过建模和假设驱动的研究,将这些和其他宿主病毒生态系统的生态和进化整合起来,并考虑出现、宿主转换/溢出以及疾病传播给人类的风险。

A Global Perspective on Hantavirus Ecology, Epidemiology, and Disease

Authors: Colleen B. Jonsson, Luiz Tadeu Moraes Figueiredo, Olli Vapalahti
Original Abstract
SUMMARY Hantaviruses are enzootic viruses that maintain persistent infections in their rodent hosts without apparent disease symptoms. The spillover of these viruses to humans can lead to one of two serious illnesses, hantavirus pulmonary syndrome and hemorrhagic fever with renal syndrome. In recent years, there has been an improved understanding of the epidemiology, pathogenesis, and natural history of these viruses following an increase in the number of outbreaks in the Americas. In this review, current concepts regarding the ecology of and disease associated with these serious human pathogens are presented. Priorities for future research suggest an integration of the ecology and evolution of these and other host-virus ecosystems through modeling and hypothesis-driven research with the risk of emergence, host switching/spillover, and disease transmission to humans.

人类汉坦病毒感染:流行病学、临床特征、发病机制和免疫学

Human hantavirus infections: epidemiology, clinical features, pathogenesis and immunology
Authors: Tobias Manigold, Pablo Vial
摘要中文翻译在人类中,汉坦病毒可引起肾综合征出血热 (HFRS) 或汉坦病毒心肺综合征 (HCPS)。目前估计每年发生 150,000 至 200,000 例汉坦病毒病病例,其中大部分发生在亚洲。然而,在美洲和欧洲,人类汉坦病毒感染的报道越来越多。尽管许多潜在的致病机制仍不清楚,但最近的证据反对纯粹由免疫介导的人类疾病病理生理学。尽管 HFRS 和 HCPS 的发病率和病死率分别较高,但目前尚无疫苗或药物被证明具有预防或治疗作用。本文总结了人类汉坦病毒性疾病的临床特征和当前流行病学发现,以及有关免疫学、发病机制和干预策略的概念。

Human hantavirus infections: epidemiology, clinical features, pathogenesis and immunology

Authors: Tobias Manigold, Pablo Vial
Original Abstract
In humans, hantaviruses can cause haemorrhagic fever with renal syndrome (HFRS) or hantavirus cardiopulmonary syndrome (HCPS). Currently it is estimated that 150,000 to 200,000 cases of hantavirus disease occur each year, the majority being reported in Asia. However, human hantavirus infections are increasingly reported in the Americas and Europe. Although many of the underlying pathogenic mechanisms still remain unclear, recent evidence rather argues against a purely immune-mediated pathophysiology of human disease. Despite the high morbidity and case-fatality rates of HFRS and HCPS, respectively, no vaccine or drug is currently proven to be preventive or therapeutic. This review summarises clinical features and current epidemiological findings, as well as concepts regarding the immunology, pathogenesis and intervention strategies of human hantaviral diseases.

拉丁美洲和加勒比地区气候因素对人类汉坦病毒感染的影响:系统评价

Influence of Climatic Factors on Human Hantavirus Infections in Latin America and the Caribbean: A Systematic Review
Authors: Kirk Osmond Douglas, Karl Payne, Gilberto Sabino-Santos, John Agard
摘要中文翻译背景:随着当前的气候变化危机及其对传染病传播的影响,人们越来越渴望了解其对全球传染病的影响。汉坦病毒在世界各地都有发现,在拉丁美洲和加勒比海地区 (LAC) 等热带地区引起肾综合征出血热 (HFRS) 和汉坦病毒心肺综合征 (HCPS)/汉坦病毒肺综合征 (HPS) 等传染病。这些地区本质上很容易受到气候变化影响、传染病爆发和自然灾害的影响。汉坦病毒是人畜共患病毒,存在于居住在拉丁美洲和加勒比地区新热带生态系统中的多种啮齿动物宿主中,并参与汉坦病毒的传播。方法:我们进行了系统评价,以评估拉美和加勒比地区气候因素与人类汉坦病毒感染的关联。根据系统评价和荟萃分析的首选报告项目 (PRISMA) 标准,在 MEDLINE 和 Web of Science 数据库中对已发表的研究进行文献检索。纳入标准包括至少 8 例人类汉坦病毒病例、至少一种气候因素以及来自 > 1 个 LAC 地理位置的研究。结果:总共有 383 篇论文符合检索标准,但有 13 项研究符合纳入标准,这些研究来自拉丁美洲的巴西、智利、阿根廷、玻利维亚和巴拿马,还有一项研究来自加勒比地区的巴巴多斯。选定的研究中使用了多种具有不同功效的数学模型,以生成与气候因素相关的人类汉坦病毒感染的可靠风险和病例估计。观察到汉坦病毒病与降水和栖息地类型因素相关的有力证据,但在温度和湿度方面观察到混合证据。结论:由于所有脊椎动物宿主宿主的身份未知、多种汉坦病毒株的传播、农业实践、气候变化和公共卫生系统面临挑战,拉美和加勒比地区气候与汉坦病毒疾病的相互作用可能很复杂。越来越需要对气候和其他相关社会、非生物和生物因素对拉美和加勒比地区传染病的影响进行更详细的系统研究,以了解新热带地区媒介传播疾病传播的复杂性。

Influence of Climatic Factors on Human Hantavirus Infections in Latin America and the Caribbean: A Systematic Review

Authors: Kirk Osmond Douglas, Karl Payne, Gilberto Sabino-Santos, John Agard
Original Abstract
Background: With the current climate change crisis and its influence on infectious disease transmission there is an increased desire to understand its impact on infectious diseases globally. Hantaviruses are found worldwide, causing infectious diseases such as haemorrhagic fever with renal syndrome (HFRS) and hantavirus cardiopulmonary syndrome (HCPS)/hantavirus pulmonary syndrome (HPS) in tropical regions such as Latin America and the Caribbean (LAC). These regions are inherently vulnerable to climate change impacts, infectious disease outbreaks and natural disasters. Hantaviruses are zoonotic viruses present in multiple rodent hosts resident in Neotropical ecosystems within LAC and are involved in hantavirus transmission. Methods: We conducted a systematic review to assess the association of climatic factors with human hantavirus infections in the LAC region. Literature searches were conducted on MEDLINE and Web of Science databases for published studies according to Preferred Reporting Items for Systematic reviews and Meta-Analyses (PRISMA) criteria. The inclusion criteria included at least eight human hantavirus cases, at least one climatic factor and study from > 1 LAC geographical location. Results: In total, 383 papers were identified within the search criteria, but 13 studies met the inclusion criteria ranging from Brazil, Chile, Argentina, Bolivia and Panama in Latin America and a single study from Barbados in the Caribbean. Multiple mathematical models were utilized in the selected studies with varying power to generate robust risk and case estimates of human hantavirus infections linked to climatic factors. Strong evidence of hantavirus disease association with precipitation and habitat type factors were observed, but mixed evidence was observed for temperature and humidity. Conclusions: The interaction of climate and hantavirus diseases in LAC is likely complex due to the unknown identity of all vertebrate host reservoirs, circulation of multiple hantavirus strains, agricultural practices, climatic changes and challenged public health systems. There is an increasing need for more detailed systematic research on the influence of climate and other co-related social, abiotic, and biotic factors on infectious diseases in LAC to understand the complexity of vector-borne disease transmission in the Neotropics.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 综述 · E1

汉坦病毒病:病毒学、临床特征和治疗方法综述

Hantavirus Diseases: A Review of Virology, Clinical Features, and Therapeutic Approaches
Authors: Chetna Baregama1, Suresh Kumar Dev1, Vijay Singh Kachawa2, Ayush Garg1, Mohammad Junaid Alam Mansoori1, Yogesh Kumar Apurva1, Vijay Kumar Bansal3, Vaibhav Rathore*4, Akhil Mangal5
摘要中文翻译汉坦病毒是世界范围内的人畜共患传染病,可引起两种主要的人类疾病:欧亚大陆的肾综合征出血热(HFRS)和美洲的汉坦病毒肺综合征(HPS)。这些疾病主要是通过吸入受感染的啮齿动物宿主的雾化排泄物而获得的,但也可能通过直接接触或与某些南美菌株进行人际传播(很少见)而发生传播。汉坦病毒会导致严重的发病率和死亡率,其严重程度取决于病毒种类、宿主免疫反应和医疗救助的及时性。过去几十年来,我们从对储存宿主分布、病毒持久性的环境决定因素以及对人类群体的溢出等方面进行的大规模研究中学到了很多知识,为了解汉坦病毒生态学提供了重要的见解。在理解分子病毒学和免疫发病机制方面也取得了重大进展,表明临床严重程度很大程度上是由于免疫介导的血管渗漏而不是直接的病毒细胞病变效应所致。然而,尽管科学上取得了进步,但仍然存在很大差距。目前还没有获得全球使用许可的疫苗,抗病毒治疗的范围和功效有限,支持性护理是临床管理的支柱。诊断方法正在改进,但在大量汉坦病毒共同传播的地区,物种特异性鉴定存在问题。此外,环境变化、城市化和啮齿动物种群动态的变化为病毒的发展和全球传播提供了更多机会。本综述涵盖了有关汉坦病毒生物学、疾病特征、临床管理、诊断方法、预防工作以及减轻汉坦病毒疾病全球负担的研究目标的现状。

Hantavirus Diseases: A Review of Virology, Clinical Features, and Therapeutic Approaches

Authors: Chetna Baregama1, Suresh Kumar Dev1, Vijay Singh Kachawa2, Ayush Garg1, Mohammad Junaid Alam Mansoori1, Yogesh Kumar Apurva1, Vijay Kumar Bansal3, Vaibhav Rathore*4, Akhil Mangal5
Original Abstract
Hantaviruses are worldwide zoonotic infections that cause two main human diseases: Hemorrhagic Fever with Renal Syndrome (HFRS) in Eurasia and Hantavirus Pulmonary Syndrome (HPS) in the Americas . These diseases are acquired mostly by inhaling aerosolized excreta from infected rodent reservoirs but transmission may also occur by direct contact or, rarely, person-to-person transmission with some South American strains. Hantaviruses produce severe morbidity and mortality, the severity of which depends on the viral species, the host immunological response, and the timeliness of medical assistance. Much has been learned over the last several decades from large-scale studies on the distribution of reservoir hosts, environmental determinants of virus persistence, and spillover into human populations that provide essential insight into hantavirus ecology. Significant progress has also been achieved in understanding molecular virology and immunopathogenesis, revealing that much of the clinical severity is due to immune-mediated vascular leakage rather than direct viral cytopathic effects. Yet despite the scientific improvements, there are still big gaps. There are no licensed vaccines for global use and antiviral treatments are limited in scope and efficacy with supportive care being the mainstay of clinical management . Diagnostic methods are improving, but species-specific identification is problematic in locations where numerous hantaviruses cocirculate. Furthermore, environmental change, urbanization, and changing rodent population dynamics are providing more opportunities for viral development and global spread. This review covers the present state of knowledge about hantavirus biology, illness features, clinical management, diagnostic methods, prevention efforts, and research objectives to reduce the global burden of hantavirus disease.

汉坦病毒和中东公共卫生挑战:主题回顾

Hantavirus and Middle East Public Health Challenges: A Subject Review
Authors: Jihad Abdulameer Ahmed, Aseel Kamil Hameed
摘要中文翻译汉坦病毒是属于汉坦病毒科的新兴人畜共患病毒,通常存在于啮齿动物宿主中,并通过接触受污染的唾液、雾化颗粒或排泄物传播给人类。有两种与人类感染相关的主要临床综合征:肾综合征出血热(HFRS)——主要报告于欧洲和亚洲,以及汉坦病毒心肺综合征(HCPS)——主要报告于美洲。中东地区汉坦病毒病发病率相对较低,但最近的流行病学研究表明,该病毒正在包括土耳其和伊朗在内的多个国家传播。随着人口的增加、气候的变化、啮齿动物的生态环境的变化、农业的扩展以及对这种情况的监测的不断完善,该地区汉坦病毒感染的出现已引起越来越多的关注。监测有限、诊断不足以及与其他发热性疾病存在大量临床重叠,所有这些都是量化区域疾病负担的重要挑战。本综述旨在总结中东地区汉坦病毒流行病学的知识状况,重点关注地理分布、环境因素、啮齿动物宿主、流行病学、临床特征、诊断方法和公共卫生准备情况。目前的数据表明,虽然人类感染很少见,但一些中东国家存在可以维持汉坦病毒的生态条件,因此需要加强对跨学科和“同一个健康”倡议的监测和研究。在医疗、兽医和环境界之间建立更紧密的联系对于未来及早发现和预防疫情至关重要。

Hantavirus and Middle East Public Health Challenges: A Subject Review

Authors: Jihad Abdulameer Ahmed, Aseel Kamil Hameed
Original Abstract
Hantaviruses are emerging zoonotic viruses belonging to the family Hantaviridae, which are typically found in rodent reservoirs and transmitted to humans through contact with contaminated saliva, aerosolized particles or excreta. There are two major clinical syndromes associated with human infections: hemorrhagic fever with renal syndrome (HFRS) — mostly reported from Europe and Asia, and hantavirus cardiopulmonary syndrome (HCPS) — mostly reported from the Americas. The Middle East has a relatively low incidence of hantavirus disease, but recent epidemiological studies have shown that the virus is circulating in several countries, including Türkiye and Iran. The emergence of hantavirus infections in this area has become a growing concern as the population increases, climate variations occur, the rodents' ecology is changing, the agriculture spreads and the surveillance of this situation is improving. There is limited surveillance, underdiagnosis, and a large clinical overlap with other febrile illnesses all of which are important challenges in quantifying the regional burden of disease. This review aims at summarizing the state of knowledge about the epidemiology of the hantavirus in the Middle East, focusing on geographical distribution, environmental factors, rodent hosts, epidemiology, clinical features, diagnostic methods and public health preparedness. Current data suggest that while human infections are rare, ecological conditions which could sustain hantavirus are present in several Middle Eastern countries, and therefore increased surveillance and research into interdisciplinary and One Health initiatives are needed. Creating closer ties between the medical, veterinary and environmental communities is crucial for early outbreak detection and prevention in the future.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 综述 · E2

探险游轮、跳岛游和人畜共患病风险:MV Hondius Andes 汉坦病毒爆发的治理和运营经验教训

Expedition cruises, island hopping, and zoonotic risk: governance and operational lessons from the MV Hondius Andes hantavirus outbreak
Authors: Esteban Ortiz‐Prado, María Paz Cadena, María A. Vethencourt-Ysea, Jorge Vasconez-Gonzalez, K O Douglas, Juan S. Izquierdo-Condoy, Esteban Ortiz-Prado, Kirk O. Douglas
摘要中文翻译与 MV Hondius 号远征邮轮相关的安第斯山脉正汉坦病毒爆发说明,当与封闭的船上环境、延迟的临床识别、远程导航、医疗疏散和国际乘客疏散相交叉时,可能的陆基人畜共患病暴露如何可能成为跨国公共卫生事件。尽管汉坦病毒感染通常与接触受感染的啮齿动物或受污染的环境有关,但安第斯病毒在正汉坦病毒中是例外,因为已记录的人与人之间的传播有限,特别是在密切和长期接触之后。本视角使用 MV Hondius 疫情作为分析案例研究,以确定探险时代旅行医学的治理和运营差距。现有的《国际卫生条例》、世卫组织船舶事件指南和 ECDC 建议为协调、通知、隔离和接触者追踪提供了重要基础;然而,这次疫情爆发暴露了探险队在安全港口准入、医疗后送、船上非特异性发热性疾病识别、诊断升级、乘客追踪和下船后监测等方面的具体差距。因此,我们提出了一种以问责制为导向的 One Health 准备模型,该模型通过路线级风险评估、暴露史评估、机上症状监测、隔离和远程医疗触发因素、参考实验室路径、停靠港协议、可审计的乘客和游览记录以及跨境旅行后监测来实施现有指南。核心教训不是探险游轮、观鸟或生态旅游本质上是不安全的,而是它们不断扩大的地理范围需要在乘客登船之前制定具有约束力、可审计且针对探险活动的疫情协议。

Expedition cruises, island hopping, and zoonotic risk: governance and operational lessons from the MV Hondius Andes hantavirus outbreak

Authors: Esteban Ortiz‐Prado, María Paz Cadena, María A. Vethencourt-Ysea, Jorge Vasconez-Gonzalez, K O Douglas, Juan S. Izquierdo-Condoy, Esteban Ortiz-Prado, Kirk O. Douglas
Original Abstract
The Andes orthohantavirus outbreak linked to the MV Hondius expedition cruise illustrates how a probable land-based zoonotic exposure can become a multinational public health event when it intersects with enclosed shipboard environments, delayed clinical recognition, remote navigation, medical evacuation, and international passenger dispersal. Although hantavirus infection is classically associated with exposure to infected rodents or contaminated environments, Andes virus is exceptional among orthohantaviruses because limited person-to-person transmission has been documented, particularly after close and prolonged contact. This Perspective uses the MV Hondius outbreak as an analytical case study to identify governance and operational gaps in expedition-era travel medicine. Existing International Health Regulations, WHO ship-event guidance, and ECDC recommendations provide essential foundations for coordination, notification, isolation, and contact tracing; however, this outbreak exposed expedition-specific gaps in safe port access, medical evacuation, onboard recognition of nonspecific febrile illness, diagnostic escalation, passenger traceability, and post-disembarkation monitoring. We therefore propose an accountability-oriented One Health preparedness model that operationalizes existing guidance through route-level risk assessment, exposure-history assessment, onboard syndromic surveillance, isolation and telemedicine triggers, reference-laboratory pathways, port-of-call agreements, auditable passenger and excursion records, and cross-border post-travel monitoring. The central lesson is not that expedition cruises, birdwatching, or ecological tourism are inherently unsafe, but that their expanding geographic reach requires binding, auditable, and expedition-specific outbreak protocols before passengers embark.

普马拉汉坦病毒感染的中枢神经系统和眼部表现

Central Nervous System and Ocular Manifestations in Puumala Hantavirus Infection
Authors: Nina Hautala, Terhi Partanen, Anna-Maria Kubin, Heikki Kauma, Timo Hautala
摘要中文翻译普马拉汉坦病毒 (PUUV) 由田鼠 (Myodes glareolus) 携带和传播,会引起一种轻度肾综合征出血热 (HFRS),称为流行性肾病 (NE)。急性高烧、急性肾损伤(AKI)、血小板减少和血尿是该综合征的典型特征。此外,头痛、视力模糊、失眠、眩晕和恶心通常与该疾病相关。本综述探讨了急性 NE 中眼部和中枢神经系统受累的机制和表现。

Central Nervous System and Ocular Manifestations in Puumala Hantavirus Infection

Authors: Nina Hautala, Terhi Partanen, Anna-Maria Kubin, Heikki Kauma, Timo Hautala
Original Abstract
Puumala hantavirus (PUUV), carried and spread by the bank vole (Myodes glareolus), causes a mild form of hemorrhagic fever with renal syndrome (HFRS) called nephropathia epidemica (NE). Acute high fever, acute kidney injury (AKI), thrombocytopenia, and hematuria are typical features of this syndrome. In addition, headache, blurred vision, insomnia, vertigo, and nausea are commonly associated with the disease. This review explores the mechanisms and presentations of ocular and central nervous system involvement in acute NE.

汉坦病毒感染相关急性炎症性脱髓鞘性多发性神经根神经病一例报告及文献复习

Hantavirus infection-related acute inflammatory demyelinative polyradiculoneuropathy: A case report and literature review
Authors: Ying Zhou, Jun Yang, Hong Hai, Jun Dong, Ying Wen
摘要中文翻译理由:肾综合征出血热(HFRS)是我国常见的传染病。作为汉坦病毒感染后的并发症,格林-巴利综合征(GBS)以前很少有报道。我们在此报道一例2023年春季汉坦病毒感染继发的急性炎症性脱髓鞘性多发性神经根神经病病例,并对既往报道病例的临床特征进行了总结。患者担忧:一名年轻男性患者主诉发烧并头痛,随后被诊断为 HFRS,血清汉坦病毒抗体 IgM 呈阳性。两周后,患者出现持续腰痛,四肢及胸腹部明显麻木,面部运动障碍,四肢肌无力。诊断、干预和结果:通过典型的脑脊液变化和肌电图检查表现,他被迅速诊断为GBS,并被证实与汉坦病毒感染有关。他接受了静脉注射免疫球蛋白治疗,然后进行康复治疗。发病后4个月内他就完全康复了。经验教训:GBS 是汉坦病毒感染的一种罕见表现。当 HFRS 病例出现急性肢体无力时,应考虑 GBS。当神经系统疾病发生时,多学科团队可以做出快速诊断和最佳治疗。

Hantavirus infection-related acute inflammatory demyelinative polyradiculoneuropathy: A case report and literature review

Authors: Ying Zhou, Jun Yang, Hong Hai, Jun Dong, Ying Wen
Original Abstract
Rationale: Hemorrhagic fever with renal syndrome (HFRS) is a common infectious disease in China. As a complication of post-Hantavirus infection, Guillain–Barre syndrome (GBS) was rarely previously reported. Here, we described a case of acute inflammatory demyelinative polyradiculoneuropathy secondary to Hantavirus infection in spring of 2023. We also made a summary of the clinical features from previous reported cases. Patient concerns: A young male patient complained a fever with headache, who was subsequently diagnosed with HFRS with positive serum Hantavirus antibody IgM. Two weeks later, he presented sustained back pain, obvious numbness located in 4 extremities, chest and abdomen, facial dyskinesia and 4 extremities muscle weakness. Diagnosis, Interventions, and Outcomes: He was rapidly diagnosed with GBS by typical cerebrospinal fluid change and the electromyography examination presentation, which was verified associated with hantavirus infection. He was treated with intravenous immunoglobulin infusion followed by rehabilitation treatment. He got a complete recovery within 4 months after disease onset. Lessons: GBS was an uncommon manifestation of Hantavirus infection. GBS should be considered when acute limb weakness happens in cases with HFRS. A multidisciplinary team could make a rapid diagnosis and optimal treatment when nervous system disorders occurred.

汉坦病毒复制周期——更新的结构病毒学视角

Hantavirus Replication Cycle—An Updated Structural Virology Perspective
Authors: Kristina Meier, Sigurdur R. Thorkelsson, Emmanuelle R. J. Quemin, Maria Rosenthal
摘要中文翻译汉坦病毒感染多种宿主,包括食虫动物和啮齿动物,也可引起人类人畜共患感染,从而导致严重疾病,并可能致命。汉坦病毒的爆发通常与宿主动物的种群动态及其靠近人类的栖息地有关,这在全球化的世界中变得越来越重要。目前,还没有批准用于人类的疫苗,也没有具体有效的抗病毒治疗方法。汉坦病毒属于布尼亚病毒目,具有三段负义RNA基因组。它们仅编码五种病毒蛋白,并在受感染细胞的细胞质中复制和转录其基因组。然而,病毒扩增周期的许多细节仍然未知。近年来,冷冻电子断层扫描、冷冻电子显微镜和晶体学等结构生物学方法为我们对病毒通过膜融合进入以及核蛋白对基因组衣壳的理解做出了重大贡献。在这篇综述中,我们提供了汉坦病毒复制周期的最新信息,特别关注结构病毒学方面。

Hantavirus Replication Cycle—An Updated Structural Virology Perspective

Authors: Kristina Meier, Sigurdur R. Thorkelsson, Emmanuelle R. J. Quemin, Maria Rosenthal
Original Abstract
Hantaviruses infect a wide range of hosts including insectivores and rodents and can also cause zoonotic infections in humans, which can lead to severe disease with possible fatal outcomes. Hantavirus outbreaks are usually linked to the population dynamics of the host animals and their habitats being in close proximity to humans, which is becoming increasingly important in a globalized world. Currently there is neither an approved vaccine nor a specific and effective antiviral treatment available for use in humans. Hantaviruses belong to the order Bunyavirales with a tri-segmented negative-sense RNA genome. They encode only five viral proteins and replicate and transcribe their genome in the cytoplasm of infected cells. However, many details of the viral amplification cycle are still unknown. In recent years, structural biology methods such as cryo-electron tomography, cryo-electron microscopy, and crystallography have contributed essentially to our understanding of virus entry by membrane fusion as well as genome encapsidation by the nucleoprotein. In this review, we provide an update on the hantavirus replication cycle with a special focus on structural virology aspects.

汉坦病毒 Gn 和 Gc 包膜糖蛋白:病毒细胞进入和病毒组装的关键结构单位

Hantavirus Gn and Gc Envelope Glycoproteins: Key Structural Units for Virus Cell Entry and Virus Assembly
Authors: Nicolás Cifuentes-Muñoz, Natalia Salazar-Quiroz, Nicole Tischler
摘要中文翻译近年来,对布尼亚病毒科三个不同属的病毒表面刺突的超微结构研究揭示了其刺突组织的显着多样性。尽管存在这种结构异质性,但在每种情况下,尖峰似乎都是由 Gn 和 Gc 包膜糖蛋白形成的异二聚体组成。在这篇综述中,总结了目前对 Gn 和 Gc 结构及其在病毒细胞进入和退出中的功能的了解。在病毒进入细胞期间,Gn 和 Gc 在受体结合中的作用尚未确定。尽管如此,生化研究表明随后的病毒-膜融合活性是由Gc完成的。此外,已经预测了汉坦病毒 Gc 的 II 类融合蛋白构象,并且新的晶体学数据证实了裂谷热病毒 (RVFV) Gc 蛋白的这种折叠。在病毒细胞退出过程中,不同病毒成分的组装似乎是通过 Gn 和 Gc 细胞质尾部 (CT) 与内部病毒核糖核衣壳的相互作用建立的。此外,最近的研究结果表明,汉坦病毒糖蛋白在病毒出芽过程中发挥着重要作用,因为它们自组装成病毒样颗粒。总的来说,这些新颖的见解为更详细地了解汉坦病毒感染周期早期和晚期的 Gn 和 Gc 功能提供了重要信息。

Hantavirus Gn and Gc Envelope Glycoproteins: Key Structural Units for Virus Cell Entry and Virus Assembly

Authors: Nicolás Cifuentes-Muñoz, Natalia Salazar-Quiroz, Nicole Tischler
Original Abstract
In recent years, ultrastructural studies of viral surface spikes from three different genera within the Bunyaviridae family have revealed a remarkable diversity in their spike organization. Despite this structural heterogeneity, in every case the spikes seem to be composed of heterodimers formed by Gn and Gc envelope glycoproteins. In this review, current knowledge of the Gn and Gc structures and their functions in virus cell entry and exit is summarized. During virus cell entry, the role of Gn and Gc in receptor binding has not yet been determined. Nevertheless, biochemical studies suggest that the subsequent virus-membrane fusion activity is accomplished by Gc. Further, a class II fusion protein conformation has been predicted for Gc of hantaviruses, and novel crystallographic data confirmed such a fold for the Rift Valley fever virus (RVFV) Gc protein. During virus cell exit, the assembly of different viral components seems to be established by interaction of Gn and Gc cytoplasmic tails (CT) with internal viral ribonucleocapsids. Moreover, recent findings show that hantavirus glycoproteins accomplish important roles during virus budding since they self-assemble into virus-like particles. Collectively, these novel insights provide essential information for gaining a more detailed understanding of Gn and Gc functions in the early and late steps of the hantavirus infection cycle.

Under Vicky's leadership, it quickly became an essential source of news, particularly at a time when major newspaper publications were increasingly overlooking local coverage. On January 1, 2022, the torch was passed to Nathan Neil and his firm, Neil Publishing, LLC. Neil, a local entrepreneur with multiple thriving businesses in Chambersburg, shares Vicky's fervent commitment to both the community and the world of local journalism. Rooted in the heart of Franklin County and powered by its residents, the Franklin County Free Press continues to bridge the gap, ensuring that the local stories, events, and issues that matter most to the community remain in the spotlight. The "xml:" Namespace This Version: 26 October 2009 Description The namespace whose name is http://www.w3.org/XML/1998/namespace is bound by definition to the prefix xml: according to Namespaces in XML (and by Namespaces in XML 1.1). Note that unlike all other XML namespaces, both the name and the prefix are specified; i.e., if you want XML 1.0 processors to recognize this namespace, you must use the reserved prefix xml:. xml:lang and xml:space As of the last update of this document, the XML 1.0 Specification (Fifth edition) (and also the XML 1.1 Specification Second edition) defines two attribute names in this namespace: xml:lang Designed for identifying the human language used in the scope of the element to which it's attached. xml:space Designed to express whether or not the document's creator wishes white space to be considered as significant in the scope of the element to which it's attached. xml:base The XML Base specification (Second edition) describes a facility, similar to that of HTML BASE, for defining base URIs for parts of XML documents. It defines a single attribute, xml:base, and describes in detail the procedure for its use in processing relative URI refeferences. xml:id The xml:id specification defines a single attribute, xml:id, known to be of type ID independently of any DTD or schema. Father denotes Jon Bosak, the chair of the original XML Working Group. This name is reserved by the following decision of the W3C XML Plenary and XML Coordination groups: In appreciation for his vision, leadership and dedication the W3C XML Plenary on this 10th day of February, 2000, reserves for Jon Bosak in perpetuity the XML name "xml:Father". Namespace change policy The XML Core Working Group reserves the right to bring additional names from this namespace into active use by providing definitions for them in new specifications or subsequent editions of existing specifications. The Working Group may also make modifications to the definitions of the names already in use, although such changes will not normally change the well-formedness or validity of existing documents or significantly change their meaning. All changes to the use of this namespace will be achieved by the publication of documents governed by the W3C Process. Other names beginning 'xml' The XML specification reserves all names beginning with the letters 'x', 'm', 'l' in any combination of upper- and lower-case for use by the W3C. To date three such names have been given definitions—although these names are not in the XML namespace, they are listed here as a convenience to readers and users: xml See the XML declaration and the XML namespace prefix xmlns See namespace declarations xml-stylesheet See The xml-stylesheet processing instruction Related Resources Section 2.10 of the XML 1.0 specification describes the syntax and semantics of the xml:space attribute. Section 2.12 of the XML 1.0 specification describes the syntax and semantics of the xml:lang attribute. An XML Schema fragment is available which constrains the syntax of xml:lang, xml:space, xml:base and xml:id in the schema language defined by the XML Schema Recommendation, Second Edition, of 28 October 2004. (An XML Schema fragment for the CR version of XML Schema is still available as well.) The value of the xml:lang attribute must be as specified by BCP 47, or be empty. The W3C XML Base specification for the xml:base attribute. The W3C xml:id specification for the xml:id attribute

正文要点中文精炼“xml:”命名空间 此版本:2009 年 10 月 26 日 描述 名称为 http://www.w3.org/XML/1998/namespace 的命名空间根据定义绑定到前缀 xml: 根据 XML 中的命名空间(以及 XML 1.1 中的命名空间)。请注意,与所有其他 XML 命名空间不同,名称和前缀都是指定的;即,如果您希望 XML 1.0 处理器识别此名称空间,则必须使用保留的前缀 xml:。xml:lang 和 xml:space 截至本文档的最后一次更新,XML 1.0 规范(第五版)(以及 XML 1.1 规范第二版)在此命名空间中定义了两个属性名称: xml:lang 设计用于标识其所附加的元素范围中使用的人类语言。xml:space 旨在表达文档创建者是否希望空白在其所附加的元素范围内被视为重要。xml:base XML Base 规范(第二版)描述了一种与 HTML BASE 类似的工具,用于定义 XML 文档各部分的基本 URI。它定义了单个属性 xml:base,并详细描述了在处理相对 URI 引用时使用该属性的过程。

The "xml:" Namespace This Version: 26 October 2009 Description The namespace whose name is http://www.w3.org/XML/1998/namespace is bound by definition to the prefix xml: according to Namespaces in XML (and by Namespaces in XML 1.1). Note that unlike all other XML namespaces, both the name and the prefix are specified; i.e., if you want XML 1.0 processors to recognize this namespace, you must use the reserved prefix xml:. xml:lang and xml:space As of the last update of this document, the XML 1.0 Specification (Fifth edition) (and also the XML 1.1 Specification Second edition) defines two attribute names in this namespace: xml:lang Designed for identifying the human language used in the scope of the element to which it's attached. xml:space Designed to express whether or not the document's creator wishes white space to be considered as significant in the scope of the element to which it's attached. xml:base The XML Base specification (Second edition) describes a facility, similar to that of HTML BASE, for defining base URIs for parts of XML documents. It defines a single attribute, xml:base, and describes in detail the procedure for its use in processing relative URI refeferences. xml:id The xml:id specification defines a single attribute, xml:id, known to be of type ID independently of any DTD or schema. Father denotes Jon Bosak, the chair of the original XML Working Group. This name is reserved by the following decision of the W3C XML Plenary and XML Coordination groups: In appreciation for his vision, leadership and dedication the W3C XML Plenary on this 10th day of February, 2000, reserves for Jon Bosak in perpetuity the XML name "xml:Father". Namespace change policy The XML Core Working Group reserves the right to bring additional names from this namespace into active use by providing definitions for them in new specifications or subsequent editions of existing specifications. The Working Group may also make modifications to the definitions of the names already in use, although such changes will not normally change the well-formedness or validity of existing documents or significantly change their meaning. All changes to the use of this namespace will be achieved by the publication of documents governed by the W3C Process. Other names beginning 'xml' The XML specification reserves all names beginning with the letters 'x', 'm', 'l' in any combination of upper- and lower-case for use by the W3C. To date three such names have been given definitions—although these names are not in the XML namespace, they are listed here as a convenience to readers and users: xml See the XML declaration and the XML namespace prefix xmlns See namespace declarations xml-stylesheet See The xml-stylesheet processing instruction Related Resources Section 2.10 of the XML 1.0 specification describes the syntax and semantics of the xml:space attribute. Section 2.12 of the XML 1.0 specification describes the syntax and semantics of the xml:lang attribute. An XML Schema fragment is available which constrains the syntax of xml:lang, xml:space, xml:base and xml:id in the schema language defined by the XML Schema Recommendation, Second Edition, of 28 October 2004. (An XML Schema fragment for the CR version of XML Schema is still available as well.) The value of the xml:lang attribute must be as specified by BCP 47, or be empty. The W3C XML Base specification for the xml:base attribute. The W3C xml:id specification for the xml:id attribute

It defines a single attribute, xml:base, and describes in detail the procedure for its use in processing relative URI refeferences. xml:id The xml:id specification defines a single attribute, xml:id, known to be of type ID independently of any DTD or schema. Father denotes Jon Bosak, the chair of the original XML Working Group. This name is reserved by the following decision of the W3C XML Plenary and XML Coordination groups: In appreciation for his vision, leadership and dedication the W3C XML Plenary on this 10th day of February, 2000, reserves for Jon Bosak in perpetuity the XML name "xml:Father". Namespace change policy The XML Core Working Group reserves the right to bring additional names from this namespace into active use by providing definitions for them in new specifications or subsequent editions of existing specifications. The Working Group may also make modifications to the definitions of the names already in use, although such changes will not normally change the well-formedness or validity of existing documents or significantly change their meaning. All changes to the use of this namespace will be achieved by the publication of documents governed by the W3C Process. Other names beginning 'xml' The XML specification reserves all names beginning with the letters 'x', 'm', 'l' in any combination of upper- and lower-case for use by the W3C. To date three such names have been given definitions—although these names are not in the XML namespace, they are listed here as a convenience to readers and users: xml See the XML declaration and the XML namespace prefix xmlns See namespace declarations xml-stylesheet See The xml-stylesheet processing instruction Related Resources Section 2.10 of the XML 1.0 specification describes the syntax and semantics of the xml:space attribute. Section 2.12 of the XML 1.0 specification describes the syntax and semantics of the xml:lang attribute. An XML Schema fragment is available which constrains the syntax of xml:lang, xml:space, xml:base and xml:id in the schema language defined by the XML Schema Recommendation, Second Edition, of 28 October 2004. (An XML Schema fragment for the CR version of XML Schema is still available as well.) The value of the xml:lang attribute must be as specified by BCP 47, or be empty. The W3C XML Base specification for the xml:base attribute. The W3C xml:id specification for the xml:id attribute

The "xml:" Namespace This Version: 26 October 2009 Description The namespace whose name is http://www.w3.org/XML/1998/namespace is bound by definition to the prefix xml: according to Namespaces in XML (and by Namespaces in XML 1.1). Note that unlike all other XML namespaces, both the name and the prefix are specified; i.e., if you want XML 1.0 processors to recognize this namespace, you must use the reserved prefix xml:. xml:lang and xml:space As of the last update of this document, the XML 1.0 Specification (Fifth edition) (and also the XML 1.1 Specification Second edition) defines two attribute names in this namespace: xml:lang Designed for identifying the human language used in the scope of the element to which it's attached. xml:space Designed to express whether or not the document's creator wishes white space to be considered as significant in the scope of the element to which it's attached. xml:base The XML Base specification (Second edition) describes a facility, similar to that of HTML BASE, for defining base URIs for parts of XML documents. It defines a single attribute, xml:base, and describes in detail the procedure for its use in processing relative URI refeferences. xml:id The xml:id specification defines a single attribute, xml:id, known to be of type ID independently of any DTD or schema. Father denotes Jon Bosak, the chair of the original XML Working Group. This name is reserved by the following decision of the W3C XML Plenary and XML Coordination groups: In appreciation for his vision, leadership and dedication the W3C XML Plenary on this 10th day of February, 2000, reserves for Jon Bosak in perpetuity the XML name "xml:Father". Namespace change policy The XML Core Working Group reserves the right to bring additional names from this namespace into active use by providing definitions for them in new specifications or subsequent editions of existing specifications. The Working Group may also make modifications to the definitions of the names already in use, although such changes will not normally change the well-formedness or validity of existing documents or significantly change their meaning. All changes to the use of this namespace will be achieved by the publication of documents governed by the W3C Process. Other names beginning 'xml' The XML specification reserves all names beginning with the letters 'x', 'm', 'l' in any combination of upper- and lower-case for use by the W3C. To date three such names have been given definitions—although these names are not in the XML namespace, they are listed here as a convenience to readers and users: xml See the XML declaration and the XML namespace prefix xmlns See namespace declarations xml-stylesheet See The xml-stylesheet processing instruction Related Resources Section 2.10 of the XML 1.0 specification describes the syntax and semantics of the xml:space attribute. Section 2.12 of the XML 1.0 specification describes the syntax and semantics of the xml:lang attribute. An XML Schema fragment is available which constrains the syntax of xml:lang, xml:space, xml:base and xml:id in the schema language defined by the XML Schema Recommendation, Second Edition, of 28 October 2004. (An XML Schema fragment for the CR version of XML Schema is still available as well.) The value of the xml:lang attribute must be as specified by BCP 47, or be empty. The W3C XML Base specification for the xml:base attribute. The W3C xml:id specification for the xml:id attribute

More Hantavirus cases reported - PIX11
正文要点中文精炼“xml:”命名空间 此版本:2009 年 10 月 26 日 描述 名称为 http://www.w3.org/XML/1998/namespace 的命名空间根据定义绑定到前缀 xml: 根据 XML 中的命名空间(以及 XML 1.1 中的命名空间)。请注意,与所有其他 XML 命名空间不同,名称和前缀都是指定的;即,如果您希望 XML 1.0 处理器识别此名称空间,则必须使用保留的前缀 xml:。xml:lang 和 xml:space 截至本文档的最后一次更新,XML 1.0 规范(第五版)(以及 XML 1.1 规范第二版)在此命名空间中定义了两个属性名称: xml:lang 设计用于标识其所附加的元素范围中使用的人类语言。xml:space 旨在表达文档创建者是否希望空白在其所附加的元素范围内被视为重要。xml:base XML Base 规范(第二版)描述了一种与 HTML BASE 类似的工具,用于定义 XML 文档各部分的基本 URI。它定义了单个属性 xml:base,并详细描述了在处理相对 URI 引用时使用该属性的过程。

More Hantavirus cases reported - PIX11

Fetched Original Body
The "xml:" Namespace This Version: 26 October 2009 Description The namespace whose name is http://www.w3.org/XML/1998/namespace is bound by definition to the prefix xml: according to Namespaces in XML (and by Namespaces in XML 1.1). Note that unlike all other XML namespaces, both the name and the prefix are specified; i.e., if you want XML 1.0 processors to recognize this namespace, you must use the reserved prefix xml:. xml:lang and xml:space As of the last update of this document, the XML 1.0 Specification (Fifth edition) (and also the XML 1.1 Specification Second edition) defines two attribute names in this namespace: xml:lang Designed for identifying the human language used in the scope of the element to which it's attached. xml:space Designed to express whether or not the document's creator wishes white space to be considered as significant in the scope of the element to which it's attached. xml:base The XML Base specification (Second edition) describes a facility, similar to that of HTML BASE, for defining base URIs for parts of XML documents. It defines a single attribute, xml:base, and describes in detail the procedure for its use in processing relative URI refeferences. xml:id The xml:id specification defines a single attribute, xml:id, known to be of type ID independently of any DTD or schema. Father denotes Jon Bosak, the chair of the original XML Working Group. This name is reserved by the following decision of the W3C XML Plenary and XML Coordination groups: In appreciation for his vision, leadership and dedication the W3C XML Plenary on this 10th day of February, 2000, reserves for Jon Bosak in perpetuity the XML name "xml:Father". Namespace change policy The XML Core Working Group reserves the right to bring additional names from this namespace into active use by providing definitions for them in new specifications or subsequent editions of existing specifications. The Working Group may also make modifications to the definitions of the names already in use, although such changes will not normally change the well-formedness or validity of existing documents or significantly change their meaning. All changes to the use of this namespace will be achieved by the publication of documents governed by the W3C Process. Other names beginning 'xml' The XML specification reserves all names beginning with the letters 'x', 'm', 'l' in any combination of upper- and lower-case for use by the W3C. To date three such names have been given definitions—although these names are not in the XML namespace, they are listed here as a convenience to readers and users: xml See the XML declaration and the XML namespace prefix xmlns See namespace declarations xml-stylesheet See The xml-stylesheet processing instruction Related Resources Section 2.10 of the XML 1.0 specification describes the syntax and semantics of the xml:space attribute. Section 2.12 of the XML 1.0 specification describes the syntax and semantics of the xml:lang attribute. An XML Schema fragment is available which constrains the syntax of xml:lang, xml:space, xml:base and xml:id in the schema language defined by the XML Schema Recommendation, Second Edition, of 28 October 2004. (An XML Schema fragment for the CR version of XML Schema is still available as well.) The value of the xml:lang attribute must be as specified by BCP 47, or be empty. The W3C XML Base specification for the xml:base attribute. The W3C xml:id specification for the xml:id attribute
